[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '# AGENTS.md\n\nThis file is the Codex-facing adapter for this repository. It summarizes the Claude-oriented project guidance from `CLAUDE.md` in a smaller format that is fast for Codex to scan. `CLAUDE.md` remains the fuller source of project knowledge.\n\n## Fast Locator\n\n- Vault root (the `~/ai-os` directory IS the Obsidian vault): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os`\n- Code repo root (this project): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/projects/lsatu`\n- Live vault knowledge: `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/wiki/` (hubs, voice, business), `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/students/`, `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/corpus/`\n- Pre-2026-07-16 LSATU product knowledge (archived, read-only): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`\n\nIf an older note says `~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/...` or `~/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/LSATU`, treat it as stale — the vault was consolidated into `~/ai-os`. Use the absolute paths above.\n\n## Project Map\n\n- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.\n- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.\n- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.\n- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.\n- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.\n- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\n\n## Stack And Commands\n\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\n- Build: `npm run build`\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\n- Test: `npm run test`\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\n\n## Working Rules\n\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\n\n## Product Constraints\n\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\n\n## Smart Drill Note\n\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\n\n## Verification (must not skip)\n\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not "done" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed "fix" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\n\n## Workflow Expectations\n\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\n- **After every successful push, publish through Lovable\'s manual Publish control.** Do not prompt the Lovable agent, use a credit-spending action, or change anything else in Lovable. Verify that the manual publish completed before reporting the change as live.\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\n\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\n\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\n\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\n\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong "Done" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\n\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\n\n## Codex Adapter Layout\n\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\n\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\n\n## Known Gaps\n\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\n\n## Agent skills\n\n### Issue tracker\n\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n\n### Triage labels\n\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\n\n### Domain docs\n\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\n\r\n'}]

## Tool: exec

const r = await tools.exec_command({"cmd":"Get-Content -Raw 'src/pages/teacher/CONTEXT.md'","workdir":"C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu","yield_time_ms":10000,"max_output_tokens":12000});
text(r.output);

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '# Teacher Workspace — Context\r\n\r\nThe teacher workspace (`/teacher`, admin-only) is Joshua\'s operator surface for running 1:1 LSAT bootcamps. This file is the glossary: what each term means here, so the nav and code stop colliding. It is not a spec.\r\n\r\n## Organizing principle\r\n\r\nThe nav is **verb-first**: organized by what Joshua does, not by artifact type. The central verb is **Assign** — pushing prepared material to a student. The long-term goal is to make assignment fast to fire and eventually automated (driven by session transcripts), pulling from a library of prepared materials.\r\n\r\n## Glossary\r\n\r\n### Assignable (material type)\r\nA kind of prepared material Joshua can give a student. These stay SEPARATE object types — they are NOT collapsed into one generic item. The types: **Bootcamp**, **Guided Drill**, **Question Set**, **Note**, **Homework**, and (future) **Whiteboard Template**. Each keeps its own builder/editor. What unifies them is not their shape but two shared surfaces: the **Library** (where all of them live) and **Assign** (how any of them reach a student).\r\n\r\n### Library (content store)\r\nThe single place that holds ALL of Joshua\'s prepared materials, grouped by type: every Bootcamp, every Guided Drill, every Question Set, every Note, every Whiteboard Template. This is a CONTENT store, not an action menu. (The current app misuses "Library" as a wrapper for assign-actions; that is the bug being fixed.) From the Library Joshua browses/edits material and can assign it.\r\n\r\n### Assign (the verb / action)\r\nThe action of pushing a Library item to one student with a date. In the redesigned Teacher Workspace, Assign is a top-level area because assigning work is one of Joshua\'s primary daily jobs. It still has **two doors**, co-equal: (1) from the **Library** — "I have this drill, send it to Lydia"; (2) from a **Student** — "here\'s Lydia, add a conditional note + the Causation bootcamp." Both doors land in the student\'s plan/feed. Future: pre-filled / automated from session transcripts.\r\n\r\n## Top-level nav (target)\r\n\r\nVerb/surface-first, co-equal Library + Students:\r\n- **Today** — the start page for what needs attention now.\r\n- **Students** — door 1: roster -> per-student plan, assign from here, recap, smartboard, conversation, that student\'s Insights.\r\n- **Assign** — the fast path for giving work to students.\r\n- **Teach** — live teaching tools, teaching materials, review surfaces, and the AI Teaching Assistant.\r\n- **Admin** — accounts + granular feature flags.\r\n\r\nHomes still TBD under these areas for: Schedule, Whiteboard, Library, Insights, Conversations, Recaps, Templates, Bootcamp References, Tutor Corpus.\r\n\r\n### Bootcamp\r\nA multi-day topic program (Causation Station, Intro to LR, etc.). It is ONE kind of Assignable. NOTE the old collision: two surfaces were both called "Bootcamps" —\r\n- the **assign** action (`TeacherBootcamps`, toggle a bootcamp on for a student) → this is just Assign.\r\n- the **analytics** view (`BootcampsHub`, per-topic completion/accuracy) → this is **Insights**, NOT Assign.\r\n"Bootcamp" the word now means only the program itself. The two jobs are named by their verb.\r\n\r\n### Insights\r\nRead-only analytics: per-student and per-topic progress, completion, drill accuracy, flashcard reviews. Absorbs the old top-level "Bootcamps" (topic analytics) and the "Analytics" Library tab.\r\n\r\n### Bootcamp Reference\r\nInspiration material **for Joshua only** (`data/bootcampReferences.ts`): example bootcamps and sources he draws from when *building* his own bootcamps. **Never assigned to students.** This is the hard line that defines the Library: References are teaching-prep for Joshua, so they do NOT belong in the Library (which holds only assignable, student-facing material). References get their own separate top-level home (a personal reference shelf). Only **Bootcamps** — the finished programs — are assignable.\r\n\r\n### Library (rule)\r\n**Library holds only things a student can receive.** If Joshua can\'t assign it to a student, it is not Library material (that rules out Bootcamp References, which are his private inspiration shelf).\r\n\r\n### Session Transcript (new term, not yet in code)\r\nThe raw record of a live lesson, produced by **Google Meet / Gemini** and **emailed to Joshua**. Should sync into the workspace automatically. It is an **ingested input**, not something Joshua writes. It is the trigger source for: (1) generating a **Recap**, and (2) future auto-suggested assignments. One transcript belongs to one student\'s session.\r\n\r\n### Recap\r\nA post-session summary for a student. **Not hand-written** — AI-drafted from the student\'s **Session Transcript**, then Joshua approves it (same approve-draft flow as Command). Lives inside the student (each recap belongs to one session). Generation is a Command/AI pipeline; there is no batch "Recaps" writing tab. A teaching output, not an Assignable.\r\n\r\n### Whiteboard (Smartboard)\r\nThe Excalidraw canvas tied to one student. It is **live and co-edited**: during a tutoring session both Joshua and the assigned student open the same board and draw/type/erase together in real time. Outside a live session it is still one student\'s board — the "teacher presents solo" case is just the student not currently looking. After the session the board **persists into the student\'s Classroom** so the student can review it later (via `posted_to_classroom` / `linked_recap_item_id`, surfaced as a `smartboard_recap` classroom item). Templates remain a teaching aid Joshua prepares once and reuses; a template is NOT student-assignable (the student receives the live/saved board, not the template). Superseded: the earlier rule that "the student doesn\'t receive a board" — that wall is removed for the live tutoring surface.\r\n\r\n### Command\r\nThe route-level home base, now named **Teacher Desk** in the product language. Command is a **task list-centered** hub for triage (what needs a reply), AI draft approvals, today\'s priorities, and the **transcript inbox** (incoming Session Transcripts awaiting recap-generation/approval). The **AI Teaching Assistant is embedded in the Desk as an always-visible panel** (see AI Teaching Assistant); the Desk and the assistant share one surface. (This intentionally reverses the earlier rule that the Desk was "not the assistant page.")\r\n\r\n### Today\r\nThe top-level start page for the Teacher Workspace. It replaces "Command" as the user-facing name for the daily home base. Today shows what needs Joshua\'s attention now and gives him a clear next action. It is organized around only three teacher task modes: Reply, Review, and Assign.\r\n\r\n### Daily Command Sheet\r\nThe desired feel of the Today page: calm, direct, and action-oriented rather than a dashboard. It emphasizes a small set of task modes and clear next actions. It avoids giant hero sections, scattered stat cards, extra category lists, and empty sections that compete with real work. The **AI Teaching Assistant panel is a permanent, intentional fixture** of the desk (not clutter), but it must still feel calm and precise rather than a busy, generic chat app.\r\n\r\n### Instrument Panel Visual Direction\r\nThe chosen visual language for the Teacher Workspace is a close adaptation of the quiet technical instrument panel reference, not a loose mood board. The page should use the same basic composition: pale graphite canvas, faint technical grid, top metadata line, compact left-side tool stack, one central wireframe work object, restrained right-side control cluster, small technical labels, thin black strokes, clear active states, and generous empty space. It should feel like a premium operating desk, not a generic SaaS dashboard.\r\n\r\n- Borrow: pale gray/blue-gray background, fine grid texture, precise black linework, compact left-side tools, restrained right-side controls, small technical labels, one selected action state, and lots of breathing room.\r\n- Translate: the central "object" becomes the selected teacher task or student decision; left tools become Today task groups and navigation; right controls become reply/assign/review/approve actions.\r\n- Avoid: copying 3D modeling controls literally, decorative cube visuals that do not serve teaching work, low-contrast text, tiny unreadable controls, and empty aesthetic space that hides the next teacher move.\r\n\r\n### Today Task Groups\r\nToday uses only three main task modes: **Reply**, **Review**, and **Assign**. These are the only task groups inside Today.\r\n\r\n- Reply: student messages or communication tasks Joshua needs to handle.\r\n- Review: drafts, uploads, transcripts, recaps, or student work waiting for Joshua\'s judgment.\r\n- Assign: work Joshua needs to give next, including suggested assignments surfaced from analytics, transcripts, or student progress.\r\n\r\nEverything else is supporting context, not a main category. Schedule appears only as a small "Next session" module. Watchlist disappears as its own category; if a student needs attention, that attention becomes a Reply, Review, or Assign task. Drafts belong inside Review. Later today is a quiet footer or side note, not a full section.\r\n\r\n### Students\r\nThe top-level area for student-centered work. It contains conversations, student detail, student assignments, student recaps, student progress, and student files.\r\n\r\n### Assign\r\nThe top-level area for giving work to students. It contains assignments, bootcamps, guided drills, activities, and templates.\r\n\r\n### Teacher Desk\r\nThe page-level name for Command. It means Joshua\'s operating desk for the teaching day: the place he starts from before opening a student, assignment, conversation, schedule, insight, or draft.\r\n\r\n### Teach\r\nA top-level Teacher Workspace area for live-session work: the surfaces Joshua uses while teaching or preparing an active session. Teach contains session-support tools such as the whiteboard, schedule/session plan, active lesson materials, AI Teaching Assistant, live notes, and transcript tools.\r\n\r\n- Not: assignments, bootcamps, guided drills, activities, or templates, which belong under Assign.\r\n- Not: student files, progress, recaps, or student detail, which belong under Students.\r\n- Not: pending drafts, uploads to review, or conversations needing reply, which belong under Today.\r\n\r\n### Study Schedule\r\nA student-specific plan of dated LSAT work or sessions that Joshua assigns from the Teacher Workspace. A Study Schedule is created in relation to one student, usually from that student\'s page or conversation, and the student\'s context is part of the assignment decision. A Study Schedule may be drafted from an uploaded PDF or HTML file, but it is not active until Joshua reviews and approves it. Once approved, it creates both a student-facing message/notification and rows in the student\'s LSAT U in-app Schedule. It does not imply writing to the student\'s personal external calendar.\r\n\r\n### Study Schedule Version\r\nAn assigned iteration of a student\'s Study Schedule. Joshua must be able to either edit the current version or replace it with a new version. Edits keep the same schedule assignment and update the affected rows. Replacements create a new version that supersedes the prior schedule. LSAT U should not infer edit-versus-replacement silently when the consequence is meaningful.\r\n\r\n### Study Schedule Cadence\r\nThe expected rhythm of a Study Schedule. Imported schedules are commonly seven-day plans or weekly templates that a student should consistently follow. The schedule should preserve day-by-day structure, recurring timing patterns, and study focus progression instead of flattening the import into unrelated calendar events. If the source contains real dates, the draft should create dated rows. If the source only names weekdays or day numbers, the draft is a weekly template and Joshua must choose the start week before assigning it.\r\n\r\n### Schedule Item\r\nOne row in a Study Schedule. A Schedule Item can have an exact time range, a loose time window such as morning or first half of the day, or only a date/day with no specific time. It should tell the student what to do that day even when no precise time exists. Time precision is part of the item and should not be invented when the source only gives a loose window. Draft Schedule Items must let Joshua edit the day/date, time or time window, task/session title, LSAT focus, instructions/notes, item type, and Needs Review status before approval.\r\n\r\n### Study Schedule Import\r\nAn approval-gated workflow that turns an uploaded schedule file into a draft Study Schedule. It is a platform workflow, not only a Study Partner feature: it can be launched from Study Partner chat, student pages, conversations, or assignment surfaces when Joshua uploads or attaches a PDF, HTML file, or other readable schedule source. The workflow should use deterministic file reading and structured parsing where possible, use AI only for ambiguous extraction or normalization, infer the intended student only when context is clear, and hold the result for Joshua\'s review. Study Schedule Import prepares the assignment; it never sends the schedule or syncs it to the student\'s LSAT U Schedule without explicit teacher confirmation.\r\n\r\n### Draft Preparation\r\nThe maximum automatic action allowed during Study Schedule Import. LSAT U may read the uploaded file, extract schedule text, parse rows, infer a likely student from context, normalize dates/times, flag uncertain fields, and organize an editable draft. Draft Preparation stops before any student-visible change: it cannot assign the schedule, notify the student, update the student\'s LSAT U Schedule, or replace an existing Study Schedule without Joshua\'s explicit approval.\r\n\r\n### Needs Review\r\nA draft state for imported schedule rows or fields that LSAT U could not read confidently. Needs Review does not mean the import failed; it means Joshua must fix or confirm the unclear parts before the Study Schedule can be assigned. Examples include missing dates, unclear time ranges, ambiguous student context, unreadable PDF text, duplicated rows, or AI-normalized fields with low confidence. Partial imports should still create a draft when useful structure is available; for example, if four of seven days are readable, LSAT U should show those rows and warn that the remaining days may be missing or unreadable. A hard import failure is reserved for files with no useful schedule structure.\r\n\r\n### Admin\r\nThe top-level area for system management and configuration. Admin contains accounts, permissions, feature flags, tutor corpus/admin review, source management that is not student-facing, and system settings.\r\n\r\n- Not: daily teaching work, student assignments, schedule preview, conversations, whiteboard, drafts needing approval, or other active teaching tasks.\r\n\r\n### Teacher Workspace Redesign\r\nThe project track for making the overall teacher workspace calmer, clearer, and easier to operate. It concerns page structure, navigation, task visibility, student access, assignment flow, analytics access, and reducing unnecessary divisions. It is related to the AI Teaching Assistant but is not the same task.\r\n\r\n### AI Teaching Assistant\r\nThe operator assistant for Joshua. It knows each student\'s context, has access to analytics, suggests next assignments, drafts student communications, helps process transcripts/notes/messages into concrete next actions, and learns from what Joshua approves, edits, rejects, and repeats. It uses a memory base to serve Joshua better across sessions. It is more than a chat box. It is **always visible as a panel on the Teacher Desk (Today)** and may also have a dedicated home; wherever it is opened it is **one assistant with one shared conversation history** (`operator_messages`, with reopenable recent chats), powered by the `teacher-operator-chat` engine.\r\n\r\n### AI Teaching Assistant Buildout\r\nThe project track for making the assistant more capable over time. It concerns drafting, recommendations, analytics-aware student context, memory, learning Joshua\'s Teaching Profile, approval-gated autonomy, and background review. It is related to the Teacher Workspace Redesign because the workspace is where Joshua primarily accesses it, but it is a distinct task.\r\n\r\n### Approval-Gated Autonomy\r\nThe operating model for the AI Teaching Assistant. The assistant should prepare drafts, actions, memory updates, and recommendations for Joshua\'s approval before anything reaches students or changes their plans. Over time, it should get smarter by analyzing Joshua\'s approvals, edits, rejections, repeated patterns, student outcomes, and teaching preferences, with the long-term aim of handling more teaching operations on Joshua\'s behalf.\r\n\r\n### Teaching Profile\r\nThe memory model the AI Teaching Assistant builds about Joshua so it can emulate his teaching operation over time. It includes Joshua\'s decision-making, teaching judgment, student-handling patterns, student communication voice, curriculum preferences, and workflow habits.\r\n\r\n- Teaching judgment: when Joshua assigns review vs. new material, slows down, pushes forward, or changes course.\r\n- Voice: how Joshua writes to students, including tone, length, directness, and encouragement style.\r\n- Student handling: how Joshua responds to low completion, confusion, missed work, strong performance, anxiety, or stalled progress.\r\n- Curriculum preferences: which drills, bootcamps, notes, explanations, and assignment types Joshua prefers for different weaknesses.\r\n- Workflow habits: what Joshua approves quickly, edits, rejects, repeats, or delegates.\r\n\r\n### Learning Source\r\nEvidence the AI Teaching Assistant can use to improve the Teaching Profile. Learning Sources include Joshua\'s explicit instructions, approved drafts, edits before approval, rejected drafts, actual student messages, recaps, assignment choices, transcript-derived notes, and student outcomes. The assistant should learn from both explicit instructions and implicit behavior.\r\n\r\n### Background Review\r\nThe AI Teaching Assistant\'s passive mode when Joshua is not actively using the page. It reviews workspace signals, organizes what it notices, and prepares suggestions for making the Teacher Workspace more efficient. It can identify tasks Joshua should consider, but it does not send messages, assign work, change plans, or alter memory without approval.\r\n\r\n### Task List-Centered\r\nThe Command page is organized by the teacher tasks waiting for Joshua, not by the student roster and not by app sections. A task answers "what do I need to handle next?" Examples: reply to students, approve assistant drafts, review uploads, assign next work, inspect analytics. Students appear inside tasks as the people affected by the work.\r\n\r\n### Task\r\nA teacher action or decision waiting for Joshua. Examples include replying to an unread student message, approving an assistant draft, reviewing uploaded work, assigning next work, investigating a progress concern, or processing a session transcript into recap/notes.\r\n\r\n- Not: a shortcut to open Students, Library, Schedule, or Insights.\r\n\r\n### Student-Centered\r\nAn organization model where the student is the primary object: Joshua scans students first, then discovers each student\'s messages, assignments, recaps, uploads, and analytics. This belongs in **Students** and student detail pages. Command may show student rows inside a queue, but Command is not primarily a student roster.\r\n\r\n### Ingestion (Drive + Gmail)\r\nThe inbound layer that feeds the workspace from Joshua\'s Google account. **Import from Google Drive and Gmail.** It supplies two things: (1) **Library material** — notes (often stored as **HTML**), question sets, files Joshua imports to assign; (2) **Session Transcripts** — Meet/Gemini transcripts that arrive by email. Materials are imported (copied in), not read live, so the Library stays the source of truth.\r\n\r\n### Note Matching (part of the pipeline)\r\nWhen a Session Transcript is processed, the pipeline also **finds Joshua\'s existing corresponding notes** (his HTML notes / materials) for that lesson\'s topic and links them to the recap/session. So the transcript pipeline is: transcript -> (a) AI-drafted Recap + (b) matched existing Notes, surfaced together for Joshua to approve and attach to the student.\r\n\r\n### The pipeline (spine of this redesign)\r\nSession Transcript (from Gmail) -> AI-drafted Recap + matched Notes -> Joshua approves in Command -> attached to the student -> (future) auto-suggested Assignments. This pipeline, not the nav cleanup alone, is the point. The IA must reserve room for it: a transcript inbox in Command, a recap+notes draft surface per student, and an "auto-suggested" state on assignments.\r\n\r\n'}]

## Tool: exec

const r = await tools.exec_command({"cmd":"rg -n -i \"Delete permanently|Revoke All Access|Grant Full Access|Archive|archive.*access|access.*archive\" src supabase","workdir":"C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu","yield_time_ms":10000,"max_output_tokens":30000});

DeskPeopleSurface() {\r\n  const { rows: students, loading: studentsLoading } = useHubStudents();\r\n  const [users, setUsers] = useState<ManagedUser[]>([]);\r\n  const [analytics, setAnalytics] = useState<AdminAnalytics | null>(null);\r\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState(\'\');\r\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\r\n  const [expandedUser, setExpandedUser] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState<RoleFilter>(\'all\');\r\n  const [singleSessions, setSingleSessions] = useState<GlobalSingleSession[]>([]);\r\n\r\n  const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {\r\n    setLoading(true);\r\n    try {\r\n      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();\r\n      if (!session) {\r\n        setUsers([]);\r\n        setAnalytics(null);\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const [usersRes, analyticsRes, singleSessionsRes] = await Promise.all([\r\n        supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', { method: \'GET\' }),\r\n        supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', {\r\n          method: \'GET\',\r\n          headers: { \'x-mode\': \'analytics\' },\r\n          body: undefined,\r\n        }),\r\n        supabase.functions.invoke(\'booking-pass-admin\', { body: { action: \'single_sessions_global\' } }),\r\n      ]);\r\n\r\n      if (usersRes.error) throw usersRes.error;\r\n      setUsers((usersRes.data || []) as ManagedUser[]);\r\n      setAnalytics(!analyticsRes.error && analyticsRes.data ? (analyticsRes.data as AdminAnalytics) : null);\r\n      if (singleSessionsRes.error) {\r\n        console.warn(\'Single Sessions list unavailable\', singleSessionsRes.error);\r\n        setSingleSessions([]);\r\n      } else {\r\n        setSingleSessions(((singleSessionsRes.data as { bookings?: GlobalSingleSession[] } | null)?.bookings) ?? []);\r\n      }\r\n    } catch (error) {\r\n      toast.error(\'Failed to load people\');\r\n      console.error(error);\r\n    } finally {\r\n      setLoading(false);\r\n    }\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    fetchData();\r\n  }, [fetchData]);\r\n\r\n  const studentById = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const map = new Map<string, HubStudentRow>();\r\n    for (const student of students) map.set(student.class_id, student);\r\n    return map;\r\n  }, [students]);\r\n\r\n  const people = useMemo<PeopleRow[]>(\r\n    () =>\r\n      users.map((user) => ({\r\n        ...user,\r\n        student: user.role === \'admin\' ? undefined : studentById.get(user.class_id),\r\n      })),\r\n    [studentById, users],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const computedAnalytics = computeAdminAnalytics(users, analytics);\r\n\r\n  const filtered = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();\r\n    return people.filter((person) => {\r\n      const matchesSearch = !q || person.email?.toLowerCase().includes(q) || person.display_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q);\r\n      const matchesRole =\r\n        roleFilter === \'all\' ||\r\n        (roleFilter === \'students\' && person.role !== \'admin\') ||\r\n        (roleFilter === \'admins\' && person.role === \'admin\') ||\r\n        (roleFilter === \'no-drill\' && person.role !== \'admin\' && !person.has_drill_access);\r\n      return matchesSearch && matchesRole;\r\n    });\r\n  }, [people, roleFilter, search]);\r\n\r\n  const toggleAccess = async (classId: string, field: string, value: boolean) => {\r\n    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => (user.class_id === classId ? { ...user, [field]: value } : user)));\r\n    try {\r\n      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', {\r\n        method: \'POST\',\r\n        body: { class_id: classId, field, value },\r\n      });\r\n      if (res.error) throw res.error;\r\n      // Trust the row the server wrote back rather than the optimistic guess,\r\n      // so a toggle that never landed can\'t look like it worked.\r\n      const written = (res.data as { profile?: Record<string, unknown> } | null)?.profile;\r\n      if (!written || written[field] !== value) throw new Error(\'Permission was not saved\');\r\n    } catch (error) {\r\n      setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => (user.class_id === classId ? { ...user, [field]: !value } : user)));\r\n      console.error(\'toggleAccess failed\', { classId, field, value, error });\r\n      toast.error(\'Failed to update permission\');\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // Puts an account back to the state a new signup is in: no intake answers, no\r\n  // name, the intake email re-armed, every paid door shut. Used to test the\r\n  // signup path for real, and when a student needs to answer the intake again.\r\n  const resetOnboarding = async (person: PeopleRow) => {\r\n    const who = person.display_name || person.email;\r\n    if (!window.confirm(\r\n      `Reset ${who} to a brand-new account?\\n\\n` +\r\n      \'Clears their setup answers and name, re-locks access, and lets the \' +\r\n      \'intake run again. Their login and their practice history are untouched.\',\r\n    )) return;\r\n\r\n    try {\r\n      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', {\r\n        method: \'POST\',\r\n        body: { action: \'reset_onboarding\', class_id: person.class_id },\r\n      });\r\n      if (res.error) throw res.error;\r\n      toast.success(`${who} is back to a new account`);\r\n      await fetchData();\r\n    } catch (error) {\r\n      console.error(\'resetOnboarding failed\', { class_id: person.class_id, error });\r\n      toast.error(\'Failed to reset the account\');\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const setRole = async (userId: string, role: \'admin\' | \'user\') => {\r\n    const prev = users.find((user) => user.class_id === userId);\r\n    if (!prev) return;\r\n    if (role === \'user\' && isPrimaryAdmin(prev.email)) {\r\n      toast.error(\'The primary admin account cannot be demoted.\');\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    setUsers((current) => current.map((user) => (user.class_id === userId ? { ...user, role } : user)));\r\n    try {\r\n      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', {\r\n        method: \'POST\',\r\n        body: { action: \'set_role\', user_id: userId, role },\r\n      });\r\n      if (res.error) throw res.error;\r\n      toast.success(role === \'admin\' ? \'Promoted to admin\' : \'Demoted to user\');\r\n    } catch {\r\n      setUsers((current) => current.map((user) => (user.class_id === userId ? prev : user)));\r\n      toast.error(\'Role change failed\');\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  if (loading) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <section className="flex h-full w-full max-w-5xl items-center justify-center border border-black/12 bg-white/25 backdrop-blur" aria-label="People workspace">\r\n        <div className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-black/20 border-t-black" />\r\n      </section>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // A room, not a floating card: the shell supplies the frame and the title, so\r\n  // this only carries its own content.\r\n  return (\r\n    <section className="flex h-full w-full flex-col" aria-label="Students">\r\n      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-black/10 px-5 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-black/45">\r\n        <Users className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n        {users.length} people\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5">\r\n        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-4">\r\n          <StatCard icon={<Users className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />} label="Total Users" value={computedAnalytics.total_users} />\r\n          <StatCard icon={<Activity className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />} label="Active Today" value={computedAnalytics.active_today} />\r\n          <StatCard icon={<Activity className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />} label="Active Week" value={computedAnalytics.active_week} />\r\n          <StatCard icon={<Crown className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />} label="Admins" value={computedAnalytics.admin_count} />\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        <div className="mt-4 border border-black/10 bg-white/20 p-4">\r\n          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">\r\n            <BarChart3 className="h-4 w-4 text-black/45" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-black/65">Feature Adoption</h3>\r\n          </div>\r\n          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-5">\r\n            {CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS.map((key) => {\r\n              const count = computedAnalytics.feature_adoption[key] || 0;\r\n              const maxAdoption = Math.max(...Object.values(computedAnalytics.feature_adoption), 1);\r\n              const pct = (count / maxAdoption) * 100;\r\n              return (\r\n                <div key={key} className="space-y-1">\r\n                  <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-black/45">\r\n                    <span>{key.replace(/^has_|_access$/g, \'\').replace(/_/g, \' \')}</span>\r\n                    <span className="font-mono">{count}</span>\r\n                  </div>\r\n                  <div className="h-1.5 overflow-hidden bg-black/10">\r\n                    <div className="h-full bg-black/45 transition-all duration-500" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />\r\n                  </div>\r\n                </div>\r\n              );\r\n            })}\r\n          </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        <details className="mt-4 border border-black/10 bg-white/20">\r\n          <summary className="flex cursor-pointer list-none items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black/65 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-black/30">\r\n            <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><CalendarDays className="h-4 w-4 text-black/45" strokeWidth={1.5} />Single Sessions</span>\r\n            <span className="font-mono text-xs text-black/45">{singleSessions.length}</span>\r\n          </summary>\r\n          <div className="max-h-80 overflow-y-auto border-t border-black/10">\r\n            {singleSessions.length === 0 ? <p className="px-4 py-5 text-sm text-black/45">No native Single Sessions yet.</p> : singleSessions.map((booking) => (\r\n              <div key={booking.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-b border-black/10 px-4 py-3 last:border-b-0">\r\n                <div className="min-w-52 flex-1">\r\n                  <div className="text-sm font-medium text-black/75">{booking.name || booking.email || \'Guest booker\'}</div>\r\n                  <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-black/45">{new Date(booking.start_at).toLocaleString(undefined, { weekday: \'short\', month: \'short\', day: \'numeric\', hour: \'numeric\', minute: \'2-digit\' })}</div>\r\n                </div>\r\n                <span className="border border-black/10 bg-white/25 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-black/45">{booking.user_id ? \'verified account\' : \'guest-owned\'}</span>\r\n                <span className={cn(\'border px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider\', booking.status === \'booked\' ? \'border-emerald-700/25 bg-emerald-700/10 text-emerald-800\' : booking.status.includes(\'failed\') ? \'border-red-700/25 bg-red-700/10 text-red-800\' : \'border-black/10 bg-black/5 text-black/45\')}>{booking.status.replace(/_/g, \' \')}</span>\r\n                {booking.meet_url && booking.status === \'booked\' ? <a href={booking.meet_url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center px-2 text-xs font-semibold text-black/65 hover:bg-white/30"><Video className="mr-1.5 h-3.5 w-3.5" />Meet</a> : null}\r\n              </div>\r\n            ))}\r\n          </div>\r\n        </details>\r\n\r\n        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">\r\n          <div className="relative flex-1 sm:max-w-sm">\r\n            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-black/35" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n            <Input\r\n              value={search}\r\n              onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}\r\n              placeholder="Search by email or name"\r\n              className="border-black/15 bg-white/30 pl-9 text-sm text-black placeholder:text-black/35 focus-visible:border-black/35 focus-visible:ring-black/10"\r\n            />\r\n          </div>\r\n          <div className="flex gap-1">\r\n            {([\r\n              [\'all\', \'All\'],\r\n              [\'students\', \'Students\'],\r\n              [\'admins\', \'Admins\'],\r\n              [\'no-drill\', \'No Drill\'],\r\n            ] as const).map(([filter, label]) => (\r\n              <button\r\n                key={filter}\r\n                type="button"\r\n                onClick={() => setRoleFilter(filter)}\r\n                className={cn(\r\n                  \'px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition\',\r\n                  roleFilter === filter ? \'bg-black text-white\' : \'text-black/45 hover:bg-white/25 hover:text-black\',\r\n                )}\r\n              >\r\n                {label}\r\n              </button>\r\n            ))}\r\n          </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        <PeopleList\r\n          filtered={filtered}\r\n          studentsLoading={studentsLoading}\r\n          expandedUser={expandedUser}\r\n          setExpandedUser={setExpandedUser}\r\n          toggleAccess={toggleAccess}\r\n          setRole={setRole}\r\n          resetOnboarding={resetOnboarding}\r\n          refresh={fetchData}\r\n        />\r\n      </div>\r\n    </section>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction PeopleList({\r\n  filtered,\r\n  studentsLoading,\r\n  expandedUser,\r\n  setExpandedUser,\r\n  toggleAccess,\r\n  setRole,\r\n  resetOnboarding,\r\n  refresh,\r\n}: {\r\n  filtered: PeopleRow[];\r\n  studentsLoading: boolean;\r\n  expandedUser: string | null;\r\n  setExpandedUser: (id: string | null) => void;\r\n  toggleAccess: (classId: string, field: string, value: boolean) => void;\r\n  setRole: (userId: string, role: \'admin\' | \'user\') => void;\r\n  resetOnboarding: (person: PeopleRow) => void;\r\n  refresh: () => void;\r\n}) {\r\n  if (studentsLoading && filtered.length === 0) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-black/10 border border-black/10" aria-label="Loading people">\r\n        {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((item) => (\r\n          <li key={item} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-4">\r\n            <Skeleton className="h-9 w-9 rounded-full bg-black/10" />\r\n            <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">\r\n              <Skeleton className="h-3 w-1/2 bg-black/10" />\r\n              <Skeleton className="h-3 w-1/3 bg-black/10" />\r\n            </div>\r\n          </li>\r\n        ))}\r\n      </ul>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (filtered.length === 0) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="mt-4 border border-black/10 px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-black/45">\r\n        <div className="flex justify-center">\r\n          <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n        </div>\r\n        <p className="mt-2">No people found.</p>\r\n        <button type="button" onClick={refresh} className="mt-3 text-xs font-semibold text-black/55 underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-black">\r\n          Refresh\r\n        </button>\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="mt-4 overflow-hidden border border-black/10">\r\n      <div className="divide-y divide-black/10">\r\n        {filtered.map((person) => {\r\n          const isExpanded = expandedUser === person.class_id;\r\n          return (\r\n            <div key={person.class_id} data-testid={`people-row-${person.class_id}`} className="bg-white/10">\r\n              <PersonRow person={person} isExpanded={isExpanded} setExpandedUser={setExpandedUser} toggleAccess={toggleAccess} />\r\n              {isExpanded && (\r\n                <PersonAccessPanel\r\n                  person={person}\r\n                  setRole={setRole}\r\n                  resetOnboarding={resetOnboarding}\r\n                />\r\n              )}\r\n            </div>\r\n          );\r\n        })}\r\n      </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction PersonRow({\r\n  person,\r\n  isExpanded,\r\n  setExpandedUser,\r\n  toggleAccess,\r\n}: {\r\n  person: PeopleRow;\r\n  isExpanded: boolean;\r\n  setExpandedUser: (id: string | null) => void;\r\n  toggleAccess: (classId: string, field: string, value: boolean) => void;\r\n}) {\r\n  const student = person.student;\r\n  const hasDrill = Boolean(person.has_drill_access);\r\n  const isAdmin = person.role === \'admin\';\r\n  const isOnline = isUserOnline(person.last_seen_at);\r\n  const lastActiveLabel = formatLastActiveLabel(person.last_seen_at);\r\n  const displayName = person.display_name || person.email;\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="grid grid-cols-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3">\r\n      <button\r\n        type="button"\r\n        onClick={() => setExpandedUser(isExpanded ? null : person.class_id)}\r\n        className="text-black/45 transition hover:text-black"\r\n        aria-label={isExpanded ? `Collapse ${displayName}` : `Expand ${displayName}`}\r\n      >\r\n        {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} /> : <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />}\r\n      </button>\r\n      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-3">\r\n        <div className="relative flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-black/15 bg-white/25">\r\n          <User className="h-4 w-4 text-black/45" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n          {student?.has_unread_from_student && (\r\n            <span aria-label="Unread message" className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-sky-500 ring-2 ring-[#c3cace]" />\r\n          )}\r\n          {isOnline && (\r\n            <span aria-label="Online on LSAT U" className="absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5 h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 ring-2 ring-[#c3cace]" />\r\n          )}\r\n        </div>\r\n        <div className="min-w-0">\r\n          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">\r\n            {/* The row opens the dossier; the chevron still expands inline for\r\n                a quick access peek without leaving the roster. */}\r\n            <Link\r\n              to={`/teacher/students/${person.class_id}`}\r\n              className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-black underline-offset-4 hover:underline"\r\n            >\r\n              {person.display_name || \'No name\'}\r\n            </Link>\r\n            <RoleBadge user={person} />\r\n            {isOnline && (\r\n              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 border border-emerald-700/20 bg-emerald-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-emerald-800">\r\n                <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-600" />\r\n                Online\r\n              </span>\r\n            )}\r\n            {student && student.assignments_total > 0 && (\r\n              <span className="font-mono text-xs font-semibold tabular-nums text-black/50">\r\n                {student.assignments_completed}/{student.assignments_total}\r\n              </span>\r\n            )}\r\n          </div>\r\n          <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-black/45">\r\n            <span className="truncate font-mono">{person.email}</span>\r\n            <span>{lastActiveLabel}</span>\r\n            <span\r\n              className={cn(\r\n                \'inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold\',\r\n                hasDrill ? \'text-emerald-700\' : \'text-black/40\',\r\n              )}\r\n            >\r\n              <span className={cn(\'h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full\', hasDrill ? \'bg-emerald-600\' : \'bg-black/25\')} />\r\n              Drill {hasDrill ? \'on\' : \'off\'}\r\n            </span>\r\n          </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </div>\r\n      <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-1">\r\n        {isAdmin ? (\r\n          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-black/40">\r\n            <Shield className="h-3 w-3" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n            Full access\r\n          </span>\r\n        ) : hasDrill ? (\r\n          <button\r\n            type="button"\r\n            onClick={() => toggleAccess(person.class_id, \'has_drill_access\', false)}\r\n            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 border border-red-700/20 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-red-800 transition hover:bg-red-500/10"\r\n          >\r\n            <UserX className="h-3 w-3" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n            Revoke Drill Access\r\n          </button>\r\n        ) : (\r\n          <button\r\n            type="button"\r\n            onClick={() => toggleAccess(person.class_id, \'has_drill_access\', true)}\r\n            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 border border-emerald-700/20 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 transition hover:bg-emerald-500/10"\r\n          >\r\n            <UserCheck className="h-3 w-3" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n            Grant Drill Access\r\n          </button>\r\n        )}\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction PersonAccessPanel({\r\n  person,\r\n  setRole,\r\n  resetOnboarding,\r\n}: {\r\n  person: PeopleRow;\r\n  setRole: (userId: string, role: \'admin\' | \'user\') => void;\r\n  resetOnboarding: (person: PeopleRow) => void;\r\n}) {\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="space-y-4 border-t border-black/10 px-8 py-4">\r\n      <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45">\r\n        Bootcamp access is controlled by assignment, not here. Assign or archive a bootcamp from the\r\n        Library to add or remove it from this student\'s path.\r\n      </p>\r\n\r\n      <div className="border-t border-black/10 pt-3">\r\n        <div className="mb-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/35">Role Management</div>\r\n        {person.role !== \'admin\' ? (\r\n          <button\r\n            type="button"\r\n            onClick={() => setRole(person.class_id, \'admin\')}\r\n            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-amber-700/25 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-800 transition hover:bg-amber-500/10"\r\n          >\r\n            <Crown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n            Promote to Admin\r\n          </button>\r\n        ) : isPrimaryAdmin(person.email) ? (\r\n          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-black/45" title="The primary admin account cannot be demoted.">\r\n            <Shield className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n            Protected primary admin\r\n          </span>\r\n        ) : (\r\n          <button\r\n            type="button"\r\n            onClick={() => setRole(person.class_id, \'user\')}\r\n            className="border border-black/15 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black/55 transition hover:bg-white/25 hover:text-black"\r\n          >\r\n            Demote to User\r\n          </button>\r\n        )}\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <div className="border-t border-black/10 pt-3">\r\n        <div className="mb-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/35">Account</div>\r\n        <button\r\n          type="button"\r\n          onClick={() => resetOnboarding(person)}\r\n          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-black/15 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black/55 transition hover:bg-white/25 hover:text-black"\r\n        >\r\n          <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n          Reset to a new account\r\n        </button>\r\n        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45">\r\n          Clears their setup answers and name, re-locks access, and lets the intake\r\n          run again. The login and their practice history survive.\r\n        </p>\r\n      </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction StatCard({ icon, label, value }: { icon: ReactNode; label: string; value: number }) {\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="border border-black/10 bg-white/20 p-4">\r\n      <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-black/45">\r\n        {icon}\r\n        <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em]">{label}</span>\r\n      </div>\r\n      <div className="font-mono text-2xl font-semibold text-black">{value}</div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction RoleBadge({ user }: { user: Pick<ManagedUser, \'role\' | \'email\'> }) {\r\n  if (user.role === \'admin\') {\r\n    return (\r\n      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 border border-amber-700/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-amber-800">\r\n        <Crown className="h-2.5 w-2.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n        {isPrimaryAdmin(user.email) ? \'Primary admin\' : \'Admin\'}\r\n      </span>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return <span className="border border-black/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-black/40">Student</span>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport default DeskPeopleSurface;\r\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\r\n\r\nconst corsHeaders = {\r\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",\r\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",\r\n};\r\n\r\n// The primary admin account can never be demoted, so the system always retains\r\n// at least one admin and can never be locked out of role management.\r\nconst PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL = "contact@aspiringattorneys.com";\r\n\r\nconst ALL_FLAGS = [\r\n  "has_bootcamp_access",\r\n  "has_classroom_access",\r\n  "has_analytics_access",\r\n  "has_schedule_access",\r\n  "has_practice_access",\r\n  "has_drill_access",\r\n  "has_waj_access",\r\n  "has_flagged_access",\r\n  "has_chat_access",\r\n  "has_export_access",\r\n  "has_ta_access",\r\n  "has_journal_access",\r\n  "has_activity_access",\r\n];\r\n\r\nasync function verifyAdmin(req: Request) {\r\n  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!;\r\n  const serviceRoleKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!;\r\n  const anonKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!;\r\n\r\n  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");\r\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) throw new Error("Missing auth");\r\n  const token = authHeader.slice("Bearer ".length);\r\n\r\n  const anonClient = createClient(supabaseUrl, anonKey);\r\n  const { data: claimsData, error } = await anonClient.auth.getClaims(token);\r\n  if (error || !claimsData?.claims?.sub) throw new Error("Unauthorized");\r\n  const user = { id: claimsData.claims.sub as string };\r\n\r\n  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey);\r\n  const { data: roleData, error: roleLookupError } = await admin\r\n    .from("user_roles")\r\n    .select("role")\r\n    .eq("user_id", user.id)\r\n    .eq("role", "admin")\r\n    .maybeSingle();\r\n\r\n  if (roleLookupError) {\r\n    console.error("verifyAdmin: role lookup failed", {\r\n      user_id: user.id,\r\n      error: roleLookupError,\r\n    });\r\n    throw new Error("Forbidden");\r\n  }\r\n  if (!roleData) throw new Error("Forbidden");\r\n\r\n  return { admin, callerId: user.id };\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction jsonRes(data: unknown, status = 200) {\r\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {\r\n    status,\r\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {\r\n    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  try {\r\n    const { admin } = await verifyAdmin(req);\r\n\r\n    // ── GET: list users + analytics ──\r\n    if (req.method === "GET") {\r\n      const url = new URL(req.url);\r\n      const mode = url.searchParams.get("mode");\r\n\r\n      // Analytics mode\r\n      if (mode === "analytics") {\r\n        const { data: profiles } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .select("class_id, last_seen_at, " + ALL_FLAGS.join(", "));\r\n\r\n        const { data: { users: authUsers } } = await admin.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });\r\n\r\n        const now = new Date();\r\n        const dayAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);\r\n        const weekAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);\r\n\r\n        const { data: roles } = await admin.from("user_roles").select("user_id, role");\r\n        const adminCount = (roles || []).filter((r: any) => r.role === "admin").length;\r\n\r\n        const activeToday = (profiles || []).filter((p: any) =>\r\n          p.last_seen_at && new Date(p.last_seen_at) > dayAgo\r\n        ).length;\r\n\r\n        const activeWeek = (profiles || []).filter((p: any) =>\r\n          p.last_seen_at && new Date(p.last_seen_at) > weekAgo\r\n        ).length;\r\n\r\n        // Feature adoption counts\r\n        const adoption: Record<string, number> = {};\r\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) {\r\n          adoption[flag] = (profiles || []).filter((p: any) => p[flag] === true).length;\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        // Recent attempts stats\r\n        const { count: totalAttempts } = await admin\r\n          .from("attempts")\r\n          .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true });\r\n\r\n        const { count: weekAttempts } = await admin\r\n          .from("attempts")\r\n          .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })\r\n          .gte("timestamp_iso", weekAgo.toISOString());\r\n\r\n        return jsonRes({\r\n          total_users: authUsers?.length || 0,\r\n          active_today: activeToday,\r\n          active_week: activeWeek,\r\n          admin_count: adminCount,\r\n          feature_adoption: adoption,\r\n          total_attempts: totalAttempts || 0,\r\n          week_attempts: weekAttempts || 0,\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Default: list users\r\n      const { data: profiles, error: pErr } = await admin\r\n        .from("profiles")\r\n        .select("class_id, display_name, last_seen_at, " + ALL_FLAGS.join(", "));\r\n      if (pErr) throw pErr;\r\n\r\n      const { data: { users: authUsers }, error: aErr } = await admin.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });\r\n      if (aErr) throw aErr;\r\n\r\n      const emailMap = new Map(authUsers.map((u: any) => [u.id, u.email]));\r\n\r\n      const { data: roles } = await admin.from("user_roles").select("user_id, role");\r\n      const roleMap = new Map<string, string>();\r\n      for (const r of roles || []) {\r\n        roleMap.set(r.user_id, r.role);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const result = (profiles || []).map((p: any) => ({\r\n        ...p,\r\n        email: emailMap.get(p.class_id) || "unknown",\r\n        role: roleMap.get(p.class_id) || "user",\r\n      }));\r\n\r\n      return jsonRes(result);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // ── POST: toggle flag, bulk actions, role management ──\r\n    if (req.method === "POST") {\r\n      const body = await req.json();\r\n      const { action } = body;\r\n\r\n      // Toggle single flag\r\n      if (!action || action === "toggle") {\r\n        const { class_id, field, value } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id || !ALL_FLAGS.includes(field) || typeof value !== "boolean") {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n        // Return the row we actually wrote. A filter that matches nothing is\r\n        // not an error in PostgREST, so without this the dashboard would show\r\n        // a successful toggle that never landed and then snap back on reload.\r\n        const { data: updated, error } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update({ [field]: value })\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id)\r\n          .select("class_id, " + field);\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {\r\n          console.error("toggle: no profile matched", { class_id, field, value });\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);\r\n        }\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: updated[0] });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Apply a template (explicit flag map) to a user. One DB update so\r\n      // the user never sits in a partially-applied state.\r\n      if (action === "apply_template") {\r\n        const { class_id, flags } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id || !flags || typeof flags !== "object") {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n        const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};\r\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) {\r\n          if (typeof flags[flag] === "boolean") {\r\n            updates[flag] = flags[flag];\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n        if (Object.keys(updates).length === 0) {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No valid flags" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n        const { error } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update(updates)\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id);\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Bulk grant/revoke all flags for a user\r\n      if (action === "bulk_user") {\r\n        const { class_id, value } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id || typeof value !== "boolean") {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n        const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};\r\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = value;\r\n        const { error } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update(updates)\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id);\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Put an account back to the state a brand-new signup is in: no intake\r\n      // answers, no name, the intake email re-armed, and every paid door shut.\r\n      // Built for testing the signup path end to end without hand-written SQL,\r\n      // and for the case where a student needs to answer the intake again.\r\n      //\r\n      // Deliberately does not touch auth: the account, its password and its\r\n      // work all survive. Nothing here deletes a student\'s practice history.\r\n      if (action === "reset_onboarding") {\r\n        const { class_id, lock = true } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n\r\n        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {\r\n          onboarding: null,\r\n          onboarding_completed_at: null,\r\n          onboarding_notified_at: null,\r\n          display_name: null,\r\n        };\r\n        if (lock) {\r\n          for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = false;\r\n          // The three a locked account still needs: somewhere to stand, a way\r\n          // to reach Joshua, and a way to book.\r\n          updates.has_classroom_access = true;\r\n          updates.has_chat_access = true;\r\n          updates.has_schedule_access = true;\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        const { data: reset, error } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update(updates)\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id)\r\n          .select("class_id, display_name, onboarding_completed_at, onboarding_notified_at");\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        if (!reset || reset.length === 0) {\r\n          console.error("reset_onboarding: no profile matched", { class_id });\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);\r\n        }\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: reset[0] });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Set role\r\n      if (action === "set_role") {\r\n        const { user_id, role } = body;\r\n        if (!user_id || !["admin", "user"].includes(role)) {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n        // Protect the primary admin: it can never be demoted, by anyone\r\n        // (including itself). This guarantees the system keeps at least one\r\n        // admin and cannot be locked out.\r\n        if (role === "user") {\r\n          const { data: target } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(user_id);\r\n          if ((target?.user?.email || "").toLowerCase() === PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL) {\r\n            return jsonRes({ error: "The primary admin account cannot be demoted." }, 403);\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n        if (role === "admin") {\r\n          const { error: upsertError } = await admin\r\n            .from("user_roles")\r\n            .upsert({ user_id, role: "admin" }, { onConflict: "user_id,role" });\r\n          if (upsertError) {\r\n            console.error("set_role: upsert failed", { user_id, error: upsertError });\r\n            throw upsertError;\r\n          }\r\n        } else {\r\n          const { error: deleteError } = await admin\r\n            .from("user_roles")\r\n            .delete()\r\n            .eq("user_id", user_id)\r\n            .eq("role", "admin");\r\n          if (deleteError) {\r\n            console.error("set_role: delete failed", { user_id, error: deleteError });\r\n            throw deleteError;\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      return jsonRes({ error: "Unknown action" }, 400);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    return jsonRes({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);\r\n  } catch (err) {\r\n    const msg = (err as Error).message;\r\n    const status = msg === "Forbidden" ? 403 : msg === "Unauthorized" || msg === "Missing auth" ? 401 : 500;\r\n    return jsonRes({ error: msg }, status);\r\n  }\r\n});\r\n\r\n'}]

Full Access\n\t\t\t\t\t\t432 image\n\t\t\t\t\t\t433 text Grant Full Access\n\nThe focused UI element is 195 button Delete permanently'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '# Tab Capability: cdp\nRaw Chrome DevTools Protocol access in browser for development use. Prefer higher-level Browser Use APIs. Navigate a fresh tab to its intended HTTP or HTTPS page before the first CDP command. Raw CDP access is scoped to the tab\'s current web origin. To observe an action, call `readEvents()` to capture `cursor`, perform the action, then read from that cursor with `afterSequence`. Continue from each returned cursor while `hasMore` is true; `truncated` means older events were evicted. Reuse the same filters while paging. Discover child target selectors from `Target.attachedToTarget` events. If you directly modify page content or browser state through CDP, outside ordinary navigation or UI interaction, and leave that change in place, tell the user what changed in the final response.\n\n```ts\nconst capability = await tab.capabilities.get("cdp");\n\ntype CdpEventsOptions = {\n  afterSequence?: number; // Return events after this cursor; omit to start at the current position.\n  limit?: number; // Maximum number of events to return, from 1 to 1000.\n  methods?: Array<string>; // Return only these CDP event methods; must not be empty.\n  target?: CdpTarget; // Filter by child target, including buffered events after it detaches.\n  timeoutMs?: number; // Wait up to this many milliseconds for the first match.\n};\n\ntype CdpCommandParams = Record<string, unknown>;\n\ntype CdpSendOptions = {\n  target?: CdpTarget; // An attached child target; omit to send the command to the tab itself.\n  timeoutMs?: number; // Maximum command wait in milliseconds.\n};\n\ntype CdpTarget = { sessionId: string; targetId?: never } | { sessionId?: never; targetId: string };\n\ninterface CdpTabCapability {\n  readEvents(options?: CdpEventsOptions): Promise<{ cursor: number; events: Array<{ method: string; params?: Record<string, unknown>; sequence: number; source: { extensionId?: string; sessionId?: string; tabId?: number; targetId?: string } }>; hasMore: boolean; truncated: boolean }>; // Read buffered CDP events, optionally waiting for the first match.\n  send(method: string, params?: CdpCommandParams, options?: CdpSendOptions): Promise<unknown>; // Send a permitted CDP command to this tab or an attached child target.\n}\n```\n'}]

{"title":"Locate deployed page bundle","code":"let tab=await cua.getTab({url:\"https://lsatprep.study/teacher/students\"},{browser:\"iab\"}); const urls=await tab.playwright.evaluate(()=>Array.from(document.scripts).map(s=>s.src).filter(Boolean)); nodeRepl.write(JSON.stringify(urls));"}

A route that\r\n   * should be public is not wrapped in this component at all.\r\n   */\r\n  allowSignedIn?: boolean;\r\n  /**\r\n   * When true, only admin users may render the children. Holding here until\r\n  // auth is definitively resolved is what makes an assignment link survive.\r\n  if (!authReady) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center">\r\n        <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin" />\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Authentication is intrinsic to this component: every shape of it requires a\r\n  // user. The check used to run only for `requireAdmin` or flagged routes, so a\r\n  // wrapper with `allowSignedIn` and no flag fell through to the unconditional\r\n  // render at the bottom and served protected content to anonymous visitors.\r\n  if (!user) {\r\n    const returnTo = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`;\r\n    return <Navigate to={authUrlWithReturnTo(returnTo)} replace />;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // After the auth checks, never before: the dev bypass opens locked features\r\n  // on a local machine, not the front door.\r\n  if (isLocalDevAccessAllowed()) {\r\n    return <>{children}</>;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Only a true cold read blocks; cached reads and background refetches keep\r\n  // this false, so gated navigations do not flash.\r\n  if (permissions.loading) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center">\r\n        <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin" />\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (requireAdmin) {\r\n    return permissions.is_admin ? <>{children}</> : <LockedModule />;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (allowSignedIn) {\r\n    return <>{children}</>;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const flags = Array.isArray(flag) ? flag : flag ? [flag] : [];\r\n  if (flags.length > 0) {\r\n    if (permissions.is_admin || flags.some((f) => permissions[f])) {\r\n      return <>{children}</>;\r\n    }\r\n    return <LockedModule />;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return <>{children}</>;\r\n}\r\n\r\n'}]

The feed flattens activities to a title and a\r\n  // subtitle; the hero needs the authored tagline and the item count too.\r\n  const { data: activities = [] } = useQuery<ActivityAssignmentSummary[]>({\r\n    queryKey: ["todo-feed:activity", user?.id],\r\n    enabled: !!user,\r\n    staleTime: 30_000,\r\n    queryFn: async () => listStudentAssignments(user!.id),\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // Every label on this page is a day-granularity comparison ("due today", "in\r\n  // four days"), so the clock is re-read once per calendar day rather than once\r\n  // per render. A fresh Date each render would invalidate every memo below and\r\n  // buy nothing.\r\n  const today = localDayISO();\r\n  // `today` is the point of the dependency, not an accident: it is the only\r\n  // thing that should make the page re-read the clock. The rule cannot see that.\r\n  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\r\n  const now = React.useMemo(() => new Date(), [today]);\r\n\r\n  /** Due dates live on classroom_items; the feed rows carry only their source id. */\r\n  const dueBySourceId = React.useMemo(() => {\r\n    const map = new Map<string, string>();\r\n    for (const item of itemsQuery.data ?? []) {\r\n      if (item.due_at && item.source_id) map.set(item.source_id, item.due_at);\r\n    }\r\n    return map;\r\n  }, [itemsQuery.data]);\r\n\r\n  const workRows = React.useMemo<HomeRow[]>(() => {\r\n    // A teacher is never the student. Verification writes and test assignments\r\n    // that name the admin account are not work; showing them as "assigned to\r\n    // you" is a lie about a list only students are supposed to own.\r\n    if (permissions.is_admin) return [];\r\n    return assigned\r\n      .filter((row) => row.status !== "completed")\r\n\r\n      // An activity row leads to /activities/:id, which is gated on\r\n      // has_activity_access. ## assistant

You were right to question it. The deployed page has the Archive and Delete controls, but the configured backend in this checkout does not implement either action.

- **Archive** sends `archive_student`.
- **Delete Permanently** sends `delete_student` after requiring the teacher to type the student’s email.
- The current `admin-manage-users` backend only supports permission toggles, templates, reset onboarding, and role changes. It has no `archive_student`, `unarchive_student`, or `delete_student` handler.

So those buttons are currently not trustworthy: they can appear and accept confirmation, but the backend configuration does not contain the action needed to complete them.

There is a second issue with the desired “completely off” behavior. Even if Archive were wired to turn every access flag off, an archived student could still sign in and reach the basic `/classroom` shell because that route intentionally allows any signed-in user. Their RRC card would disappear, since RRC is gated by `has_activity_access`, but this is not a total lockout.

No student records were changed. The required fix is to add real archive/unarchive/delete handlers to the deployed function, ensure Archive clears all feature flags, and decide whether Archive should also block sign-in entirely or merely leave the student on the empty/intake-style classroom shell.

## My request:
Okay. I want you to make it work and ensure it works properly. And yes, Archive should also block sign entirely. It should.


## assistant

I’ll implement Archive as a genuine account lockout and make permanent deletion remove the account and its student-owned data. I’ll add regression coverage for both flows, then verify the UI behavior locally.
