[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '---\nname: codex-build\ndescription: "Hand a frozen spec (PLAN.md or any locked plan) to OpenAI Codex to IMPLEMENT with full write access, while Codex stays the spec-writer and reviewer — the exact role-flip of /codex-review. Codex builds from the spec in a --yolo sandbox, Codex reads the full diff like a contributor PR, runs the proof test, and iterates fixes via the SAME Codex session up to MAX_FIX_ROUNDS before taking over. Human approves the diff before any commit. Use when the user says \\"/codex-build\\", \\"have codex build this\\", \\"codex implement the plan\\", \\"hand the plan to codex\\", \\"delegate the build to codex\\", or right after a plan survives /grill-me-codex, /grill-with-docs-codex, or /codex-review and they choose Codex for implementation (Act 3). Also for standalone delegation: refactors, mechanical migrations, bug fixes with a known repro, test/coverage writing — anything that reads as a work order. NOT for tiny edits (~<20 lines — delegation overhead loses), NOT for design work (if writing the spec forces decisions, that\'s /grill-me-codex first), NOT for reviewing existing code (/codex:review), and NOT for anything needing Codex-session tools (MCP, secrets, browser)."\n---\n\n# Codex-Build — Codex Types, Codex Verifies\n\nThe role-flip of `/codex-review`: there, Codex builds the plan and Codex critiques read-only. Here, **Codex is the builder with write access; Codex is the spec-writer and reviewer.** Codex implements a frozen spec end-to-end; Codex judges the diff like a contributor PR, demands proof, and iterates fixes in the same Codex session. The human enters at exactly two points: kickoff and diff sign-off.\n\nAdapted from Peter Steinberger\'s `codex-first` pattern (agent-scripts), rebuilt on this house\'s verified Codex mechanics.\n\n**Spec quality decides success.** Codex starts with zero session context — everything it needs must be in the prompt. A plan that survived `/grill-me-codex` or `/codex-review` already is a frozen spec; that\'s the ideal input.\n\n## Prerequisites (verify once, fast)\n\n- `codex --version` ≥ 0.130 (older CLIs error on the default `gpt-5.5` model).\n- Codex authenticated (prior `codex login`; ChatGPT account is fine). On auth/model error, surface it — don\'t silently retry.\n- Do NOT pin `-m` or model config (e.g. `model_reasoning_effort`) unless the user asks. Pinning `gpt-5.x-codex` variants 400s on ChatGPT-account auth; config defaults come from `~/.codex/config.toml`.\n- **Echo the active model at kickoff** so the user can confirm: read the `model` line from `~/.codex/config.toml` (absent = "CLI default"); state it with the resolved tunables. If the user objects, stop before launching the build.\n- **Codex has a native image-generation tool** in `codex exec` sessions (ChatGPT-account backed, no API key; verified 2026-07-08 — it saved a generated PNG to disk headless). Specs may therefore include "generate these image assets yourself" steps: name exact file paths, dimensions, and style in the prompt contract.\n- Run from the target repo\'s root (both `exec` and `resume` then need no `-C`; `resume` doesn\'t support `-C` anyway).\n\n## Tunables (read from args, else default)\n\n| Var | Default | Meaning |\n|-----|---------|---------|\n| `SPEC_FILE` | `PLAN.md` | The frozen spec Codex implements. |\n| `MAX_FIX_ROUNDS` | `2` | Fix iterations via resume before Codex takes over and finishes directly. |\n| `LOG_FILE` | `PLAN-REVIEW-LOG.md` | Append-only build transcript. If it exists (Act 1/2 ran), append `## Act 3 — Build`; else create it. |\n| `PROOF_CMD` | from spec | Exact test/verify command Codex must run as proof. If the spec lacks one, ask the user ONE question to get it before launching. |\n\nEcho resolved values before starting.\n\n## Step 0 — Gates (before any Codex launch)\n\n1. **Spec gate.** `SPEC_FILE` must exist and read as a work order (goal, concrete steps, bounds). No spec → offer `/grill-me-codex` (interview first) or `/codex-review` (have a plan, want it stress-tested) instead. If the user insists on building from a rough idea, write the spec WITH them first — that\'s design, and design stays with Codex.\n2. **Clean-tree gate.** `git status -sb`. Dirty working tree → STOP and ask the user to commit or stash first. Non-negotiable: Codex writes with full access, and a dirty tree means its diff can\'t be isolated or cleanly reverted.\n3. Confirm scope in one line, then go. No round-by-round approvals; the human gate is at the end.\n\n## Step 1 — The build prompt (contract, via temp file)\n\nNever inline-quote the prompt — write it to a temp file. Fill this contract completely; when chained from a grill/review skill, derive it from the plan\'s sections:\n\n```bash\nP=$(mktemp)\ncat >"$P" <<\'EOF\'\nGOAL: <one paragraph — what done looks like>\nSPEC: Read <SPEC_FILE> at the repo root. It is a frozen, already-reviewed spec.\n  Implement it exactly. If a step is impossible as written, implement the\n  closest faithful version and report the deviation — do not redesign.\nKEY PATHS: <files/dirs Codex will touch or must read first>\nCONSTRAINTS: <"don\'t touch X", style rules, deps that must not change>\nNON-GOALS: <explicitly out of scope — from the plan\'s Out of scope section>\nPROOF: Run `<PROOF_CMD>` and include its full output in your report.\nOUTPUT: End with a report — files changed (one line each: path + what/why),\n  proof output, and any deviations from the spec with reasons.\nEOF\n```\n\n## Step 2 — Launch Codex (fresh session, capture `thread_id`)\n\n```bash\ncodex exec --yolo --json -o /tmp/codex-build.txt - <"$P" 2>/dev/null | grep \'"type":"thread.started"\'\n```\n\n- Prompt goes via stdin (`- <"$P"`) — this both avoids quoting bugs AND sidesteps the non-TTY stdin hang (`codex exec` blocks forever waiting on stdin EOF under Codex\'s Bash tool; feeding the file gives immediate EOF).\n- Parse `thread_id` from the `{"type":"thread.started","thread_id":"..."}` line → `THREAD_ID`. Codex\'s final report lands in `/tmp/codex-build.txt` — read that file; don\'t parse the JSONL stream for content.\n- `2>/dev/null` suppresses cosmetic MCP/auth stderr noise. Confirm success by the report file + a `thread.started` line; neither → failed run (auth/model) — stop and tell the user.\n- **Timing:** foreground with `timeout: 600000` on the Bash tool call (default 2-min tool timeout kills real builds). If the spec is clearly >10 min of work (multi-file feature, migration, anything with image generation), launch with `run_in_background: true` instead and read the `-o` file when it exits. Don\'t kill a quiet background run early — Codex builds are legitimately slow.\n- **Heads-up on completion (required):** when a background Codex run finishes, the FIRST line of your next message to the user must be a loud standalone banner — `🔔 CODEX FINISHED — <what> (exit ok/fail) — verifying now` — BEFORE any verification output. The user is not watching tool calls; never let a completed build slide silently into the verify phase.\n\n## Step 3 — Verify (Codex, always, never delegated)\n\nCodex\'s report is advisory. Verify yourself:\n\n1. `git status -sb` + read the FULL diff (`git diff`). Judge it like a contributor PR: correctness, spec fidelity, style match with surrounding code, nothing touched outside scope.\n2. Run `PROOF_CMD` yourself (or the focused tests for the changed area). Codex\'s pasted output doesn\'t count as proof.\n3. Append to `LOG_FILE` under `## Act 3 — Build`: `### Round <n> — Codex build` + its report summary + `### Codex\'s verdict` + what passed/failed review.\n\n## Step 4 — Fix loop (same session, bounded)\n\nProblems found → resume the SAME session (Codex keeps its context; cheaper and better than a fresh run). Write the fix list to a temp file (`$P2`), same contract discipline: exact problem, exact file, proof expected.\n\n```bash\n# resume has no --yolo and no -C: run from the repo dir and spell the long flag,\n# or Codex inherits config.toml\'s sandbox (possibly read-only) and can\'t write.\ncodex exec resume "$THREAD_ID" --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox --json \\\n  -o /tmp/codex-build.txt - <"$P2" 2>/dev/null >/dev/null\n```\n\nRe-verify (Step 3) after each round. After `MAX_FIX_ROUNDS` failed rounds: STOP delegating — Codex takes over and finishes the remaining fixes directly. Log the takeover. Ping-ponging trivia through delegation burns more than it saves.\n\n## Step 5 — Human gate (diff sign-off)\n\nPresent: 3-bullet summary of what was built, files-changed list, proof-test output (pass/fail, verbatim tail), rounds used, any spec deviations. Ask: *"Codex built it, proof passes, diff reviewed. Commit?"*\n\n- Commit ONLY on yes — and Codex writes the commit, never Codex.\n- Rejected → ask what\'s wrong, route back to Step 4 (or take over directly if fix rounds are spent).\n\n## Hard rules\n\n- Clean tree before launch. Always. No exceptions.\n- Codex never skips the diff read. Codex claims are advisory until Codex has read the diff and run the proof.\n- Fix loop terminates at `MAX_FIX_ROUNDS` — then Codex takes over. No unbounded delegation ping-pong.\n- Commits, pushes, releases, GitHub mutations: Codex-side only, after the human gate. Codex never commits.\n- `LOG_FILE` is the deliverable — with Acts 1/2 it tells the whole story: grilled → reviewed → built → verified.\n\n## What NOT to do\n\n- Don\'t build without a spec — that\'s designing by delegation, and it fails. Route to `/grill-me-codex` or `/codex-review` first.\n- Don\'t use for ~<20-line single-obvious-change edits — just make the edit.\n- Don\'t pin `-codex` model variants on ChatGPT-account auth — 400s.\n- Don\'t resume with `--last` — capture and use the explicit `THREAD_ID` (parallel sessions make `--last` grab the wrong thread). And ECHO the id into the command visibly before running: `resume` with a missing/garbage id can silently fall back to the most recent session instead of erroring (observed 2026-07-08) — a wrong-target resume looks exactly like a successful one.\n- Don\'t parse the JSONL stream for the report — read the `-o` file.\n- Don\'t let Codex commit, and don\'t auto-commit yourself — human gate first.\n\r\n'}]

Use the `update()` method if you have data which rarely changes or is specific only to the signed-in user.\n\n## Examples\n\n### Update the email for an authenticated user\n    \n    `const { user, error } = await supabase.auth.update({email: \'new@email.com\'})\n    `\n\n### Update the password for an authenticated user\n    \n    `const { user, error } = await supabase.auth.update({password: [REDACTED] password\'})\n    `\n\n### Update the user\'s metadata\n    \n    `const { user, error } = await supabase.auth.update({\n      data: { hello: \'world\' }\n    })\n    `--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nSwift: Update a user | Supabase Docs (https://supabase.com/docs/reference/swift/auth-admin-updateuserbyid)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn0search5\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: today;     `let user = try await supabase.auth.admin.updateUserById(\n\n# Swift: Update a user\n\nUpdate user by ID.\n\n## Examples\n\n### Update user by ID\n    \n    `let user = try await supabase.auth.admin.updateUserById(\n      "715ed5db-f090-4b8c-a067-640ecee36aa0",\n      attributes: AdminUserAttributes(\n        email: "newemail@email.com"\n      )\n    )--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nJavaScript: createUser | Supabase Docs (https://supabase.com/docs/reference/javascript/auth-admin-createuser)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn0search6\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: today;     `const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.admin.createUser({ ...     `const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.admin.createUser({\n\n# JavaScript: createUser\n\nCreates a new user. You cannot delete a user if they are the owner of any objects in Supabase Storage. ... When the goal is to remove an account so it can no longer access your app, delete the auth user with `auth.admin.deleteUser()`. ...   * A temporary ban only blocks sign-in for its duration and does not revoke existing sessions.\n\nAuth\n\n# \n\nUser Management\n\nView, delete, and export user information.\n\nYou can view your users on the Users page of the Dashboard. You can also view the contents of the Auth schema in the Table Editor.\n\n## Accessing user data via API#\n\nFor security, the Auth schema is not exposed in the auto-generated API. If you want to access users data via the API, you can create your own user tables in the `public` schema.\n\nMake sure to protect the table by enabling Row Level Security and only granting the necessary privileges for each role. Reference the `auth.users` table to ensure data integrity. Specify `on delete cascade` in the reference. For example, a `public.profiles` table might look like this:\n    \n    create table public.profiles (\n    \n    2\n    \n      id uuid not null references auth.users on delete cascade,\n    \n    3\n    \n      first_name text,\n    \n    4\n    \n      last_name text,\n    \n    5\n    \n    6\n    \n      primary key (id)\n    \n    7\n    \n    );\n    \n    8\n    \n    9\n    \n    -- Grant the privileges the roles need\n    \n    10\n    \n    GRANT SELECT ON public.profiles TO anon;\n    \n    11\n    \n    GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.profiles TO authenticated;\n    \n    12\n    \n    GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.profiles TO service_role;\n    \n    13\n    \n    14\n    \n    -- Enable row level security for the table\n    \n    15\n    \n    alter table public.profiles enable row level security;\n    \n    `\n\nOnly use primary keys as foreign key references for schemas and tables like `auth.users` which are managed by Supabase. Postgres lets you specify a foreign key reference for columns backed by a unique index (not necessarily primary keys).\n\nPrimary keys are guaranteed not to change. Columns, indices, constraints or other database objects managed by Supabase may change at any time and you should be careful when referencing them directly.\n\nTo update your `public.profiles` table every time a user signs up, set up a trigger. If the trigger fails, it could block signups, so test your code thoroughly.\n    \n    -- inserts a row into public.profiles\n    \n    2\n    \n    create function public.handle_new_user()\n    \n    3\n    \n    returns trigger\n    \n    4\n    \n    language plpgsql\n    \n    5\n    \n    security definer set search_path = \'\'\n    \n    6\n    \n    as $$\n    \n    7\n    \n    begin\n    \n    8\n    \n      insert into public.profiles (id, first_name, last_name)\n    \n    9\n    \n      values (new.id, new.raw_user_meta_data ->> \'first_name\', new.raw_user_meta_data ->> \'last_name\');\n    \n    10\n    \n      return new;\n    \n    11\n    \n    end;\n    \n    12\n    \n    $$;\n    \n    13\n    \n    14\n    \n    -- trigger the function every time a user is created\n    \n    15\n    \n    create trigger on_auth_user_created\n    \n    16\n    \n      after insert on auth.users\n    \n    17\n    \n      for each row execute procedure public.handle_new_user();\n    \n    `\n\n## Adding and retrieving user metadata#\n\nYou can assign metadata to users on sign up:\n    \n    1\n    \n    import { createClient } from \'@supabase/supabase-js\'\n    \n    2\n    \n    const supabase = createClient(process.env.SUPABASE_URL!, process.env.SUPABASE_KEY!)\n    \n    3\n    \n    4\n    \n    // ---cut---\n    \n    5\n    \n    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({\n    \n    6\n    \n      email: \'valid.email@supabase.io\',\n    \n    7\n    \n      password: [REDACTED],\n    \n    8\n    \n      options: {\n    \n    9\n    \n        data: {\n    \n    10\n    \n          first_name: \'John\',\n    \n    11\n    \n          age: 27,\n    \n    12\n    \n        },\n    \n    13\n    \n      },\n    \n    14\n    \n    })\n    \n    `\n\nUser metadata is stored on the `raw_user_meta_data` column of the `auth.users` table. To view the metadata:\n    \n    1\n    \n    import { createClient } from \'@supabase/supabase-js\'\n    \n    2\n    \n    const supabase = createClient(process.env.SUPABASE_URL!, process.env.SUPABASE_KEY!)\n    \n    3\n    \n    4\n    \n    // ---cut---\n    \n    5\n    \n    const {\n    \n    6\n    \n      data: { user },\n    \n    7\n    \n    } = await supabase.auth.getUser()\n    \n    8\n    \n    let metadata = user?.user_metadata\n    \n    `\n\n## Deleting users#\n\nYou may delete users directly or via the management console at Authentication > Users. Note that deleting a user from the `auth.users` table does not automatically sign out a user. As Supabase makes use of JSON Web Tokens (JWT), a user\'s JWT will remain "valid" until it has expired.\n\nYou cannot delete a user if they are the owner of any objects in Supabase Storage.\n\nYou will encounter an error when you try to delete an Auth user that owns any Storage objects. If this happens, try deleting all the objects for that user, or reassign ownership to another user.\n\n### Removing account access#\n\nWhen the goal is to remove an account so it can no longer access your app, delete the auth user with `auth.admin.deleteUser()`. With the default `shouldSoftDelete: false`, this removes the row from `auth.users`, which cascades to `auth.sessions` and invalidates the user\'s refresh tokens — so the account can no longer mint new access tokens.\n\nA few things are not a substitute for deleting the user:\n\n  * A temporary ban only blocks sign-in for its duration and does not revoke existing sessions.\n  * Marking the account as deleted only in your own application tables leaves the `auth.users` row intact, so it can still authenticate and refresh.\n\nDeleting the user still cannot retroactively invalidate an access token that was already issued. Supabase access tokens are stateless JWTs, so a token already in the user\'s hands stays valid until its `exp` claim passes, and during that window the account can still call the API. You have two ways to handle this window:\n\n  * Bound it: keep the access token (JWT) expiry short, so any outstanding token expires soon after you delete the user.\n  * Close it for sensitive operations: validate the `session_id` claim against the `auth.sessions` table on those operations. Because deleting the user removes the session row, an outstanding token fails this check — but only on the requests where you perform it; other API calls still accept the token until `exp`.\n\nSee User sessions for details.\n\n## Exporting users#\n\nAs Supabase is built on top of Postgres, you can query the `auth.users` and `auth.identities` table via the `SQL Editor` tab to extract all users:\n    \n    `\n    \n    1\n    \n    select * from auth.users;\n    \n    `\n\nYou can then export the results as CSV.--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nJavaScript: Overview | Supabase Docs (https://supabase.com/docs/reference/javascript/auth)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn0search8\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: today; # JavaScript: Overview  * The auth methods can be accessed via the `supabase.auth` namespace.\n\n# JavaScript: Overview\n\n  * The auth methods can be accessed via the `supabase.auth` namespace.\n\n  * By default, the supabase client sets `persistSession` to true and attempts to store the session in local storage. When using the supabase client in an environment that doesn\'t support local storage, you might notice the following warning message being logged:\n\n> No storage option exists to persist the session, which may result in unexpected behavior when using auth. If you want to set `persistSession` to true, please provide a storage option or you may set `persistSession` to false to disable this warning.\n\nThis warning message can be safely ignored if you\'re not using auth on the server-side. If you are using auth and you want to set `persistSession` to true, you will need to provide a custom storage implementation that follows this interface.\n\n  * Any email links and one-time passwords (OTPs) sent have a default expiry of 24 hours. We have the following rate limits in place to guard against brute force attacks.\n\n  * The expiry of an access token can be set in the "JWT expiry limit" field in your project\'s auth settings. A refresh token never expires and can only be used once.\n\n## Examples\n\n### Create auth client\n    \n    `import { createClient } from \'@supabase/supabase-js\'\n    \n    const supabase = createClient(supabase_url, publishable_key)\n    `\n\n### Create auth client (server-side)\n    \n    `import { createClient } from \'@supabase/supabase-js\'\n    \n    const supabase = createClient(supabase_url, publishable_key, {\n      auth: {\n        autoRefreshToken: false,\n        persistSession: false,\n        detectSessionInUrl: false\n      }\n    })\n    `--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nPython: Update a user | Supabase Docs (https://supabase.com/docs/reference/python/auth-admin-updateuserbyid)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn0search9\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: today;     `response = supabase.auth.admin.update_user_by_id(\n\n# Python: Update a user\n\n## Parameters\n\n  * ### uid\n\n(Required)\n\nundefined\n\n  * ### attributes\n\n(Required)\n\nThe data you want to update. This function should only be called on a server. To only send a confirmation link to the user\'s new email, disable Secure email change in your project\'s email auth provider settings.\n\n## Examples\n\n### Update the email for an authenticated user\n    \n    `var attrs = new UserAttributes { Email = "new-email@example.com" };\n    var response = await supabase.Auth.Update(attrs);\n    `\n\n### Update the password for an authenticated user\n    \n    `var attrs = new UserAttributes { Password = [REDACTED] };\n    var response = await supabase.Auth.Update(attrs);\n    `\n\n### Update the user\'s metadata\n    \n    `var attrs = new UserAttributes\n    {\n      Data = new Dictionary<string, string> { {"example", "data" } }\n    };\n    var response = await supabase.Auth.Update(attrs);\n    `--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nDATA PROCESSING ADDENDUM (https://supabase.com/downloads/docs/Supabase%2BDPA%2B231211.pdf)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn0search12\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Published: 1.6 years ago; User Authentication for Supabase internal resources is protected with both a strong\nThe\nplan is regularly tested and updated.\nAccess controls\nSupabase limits access to personal data by implementing appropriate access controls, including the\nfollowing:\n●\nAccess to infrastructure and internal resources is managed on the basis of the Principle of\nLeast Privilege: individuals are granted only the privileges they require to execute their\nbusiness duties, and said privilege is revoked when it is no longer needed.\n●\nAccess management is centralized to identity providers, and wherever feasible, internal\nservice delegate both authentication and authorization to these providers. This ensures that\noff-boarding and privilege revocation can be handled in a timely fashion.\n●\nSupabase infrastructure requires approvals from at least one additional authorized person\nbefore any changes can be made. Authorized persons are designated based on the\nrelevance of the system in question to their business roles.\n●\nUser Authentication for Supabase internal resources is protected with both a strong\npassword policy, as well as mandatory 2FA that disallows the use of SMS-based 2FA .\n12--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nSupabase DPA (March 12, 2026).docx (https://supabase.com/downloads/docs/Supabase%2BDPA%2B260317.pdf)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn0search13\ue201 [wordlim: 200] <td>Supabase Pte. ... <td>Authorized Users account authentication</td>\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'JavaScript: signOut | Supabase Docs (https://supabase.com/docs/reference/javascript/auth-signout)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn1search0\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: today; Inside a browser context, `signOut()` will remove the logged in user from the browser session and log them out - removing all items from localstorage and then trigger a `"SIGNED_OUT"` event.For server-side management, you can revoke all refresh tokens for a user by passing a user\'s JWT through to `auth.api.signOut(JWT: string)`. ...     `const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut()\n\n# JavaScript: signOut\n\nInside a browser context, `signOut()` will remove the logged in user from the browser session and log them out - removing all items from localstorage and then trigger a `"SIGNED_OUT"` event.\n\nFor server-side management, you can revoke all refresh tokens for a user by passing a user\'s JWT through to `auth.api.signOut(JWT: string)`. There is no way to revoke a user\'s access token jwt until it expires. It is recommended to set a shorter expiry on the jwt for this reason.\n\nIf using `others` scope, no `SIGNED_OUT` event is fired!\n\nWarning: the default `scope` is `\'global\'`. This signs the user out of every device they are currently signed in on, not just the current tab/session. If you only want to sign the user out of the current session (the behavior most other auth libraries default to), pass `{ scope: \'local\' }` explicitly.\n\n## Parameters\n\n  * ### options\n\n(Required)\n\n## Examples\n\n### Sign out of every device (global – default)\n    \n    `const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut()\n    `\n\n### Sign out only the current session (recommended for most apps)\n    \n    `const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut({ scope: \'local\' })\n    `\n\n### Sign out of all other sessions, keep the current one\n    \n    `const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut({ scope: \'others\' })\n    `--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nJavaScript: signOut | Supabase Docs (https://supabase.com/docs/reference/javascript/auth-admin-signout)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn1search1\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: today; # JavaScript: signOutRemoves a logged-in session.\n\n# JavaScript: signOut\n\nRemoves a logged-in session.\n\n## Parameters\n\n  * ### jwt\n\n(Required)\n\nA valid, logged-in JWT.\n\n  * ### scope\n\n(Required)\n\nThe logout sope.--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nSigning out | Supabase Docs (https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/signout)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn1search2\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Published: today; Crawled: today; Supabase Auth allows you to specify three different scopes for when a user invokes the sign out API in your application: ... With the `global` scope, calling `signOut()` on one device revokes the refresh tokens for every session that user has, including other devices and browsers.\n\nAuth\n\n# \n\nSigning out\n\nSigning out a user\n\nSigning out a user works the same way no matter what method they used to sign in.\n\nCall the sign out method from the client library. It removes the active session and clears Auth data from the storage medium.\n\nJavaScript Dart Swift Kotlin Python C#\n    \n    `\n    \n    1\n    \n    async function [Button: signOut]() {\n    \n    2\n    \n      const { [Button: error] } = await [Button: supabase].[Button: auth].[Button: signOut]()\n    \n    3\n    \n    }\n    \n    `\n\n## Sign out and scopes#\n\nSupabase Auth allows you to specify three different scopes for when a user invokes the sign out API in your application:\n\n  * `global` (default) when all sessions active for the user are terminated.\n  * `local` which only terminates the current session for the user but keep sessions on other devices or browsers active.\n  * `others` to terminate all but the current session for the user.\n\nYou can invoke these by providing the `scope` option:\n\nJavaScript, Swift, Python, and C# default to the `global` scope. Dart and Kotlin default to `local`.\n\nWith the `global` scope, calling `signOut()` on one device revokes the refresh tokens for every session that user has, including other devices and browsers. Those other sessions fail with `AuthApiError: Invalid Refresh Token: Refresh Token Not Found` the next time they try to refresh, even though no one signed out on that device.\n\nIf your app expects independent sessions per device, pass the `local` scope explicitly, as shown in the examples below.\n\nJavaScript Dart Kotlin C#\n    \n    `\n    \n    1\n    \n    // defaults to the global scope\n    \n    2\n    \n    await [Button: supabase].[Button: auth].[Button: signOut]()\n    \n    3\n    \n    4\n    \n    // sign out from the current session only\n    \n    5\n    \n    await [Button: supabase].[Button: auth].[Button: signOut]({ [Button: scope]: \'local\' })\n    \n    `\n\nUpon sign out, all refresh tokens and potentially other database objects related to the affected sessions are destroyed and the client library removes the session stored in the local storage medium.\n\nAccess Tokens of revoked sessions remain valid until their expiry time, encoded in the `exp` claim. The user won\'t be immediately logged out and will only be logged out when the Access Token expires.--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nJavaScript: deleteUser | Supabase Docs (https://supabase.com/docs/reference/javascript/auth-admin-deleteuser)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn1search3\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: today; # JavaScript: deleteUser ...     `const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.admin.deleteUser(\n\n# JavaScript: deleteUser\n\nDelete a user. The client calls it automatically during construction, and to react to sign-in events (including post-redirect events) you should subscribe to `onAuthStateChange` rather than awaiting `initialize()`.\n\nYou only need to call it manually when you have opted out of the automatic call by passing `skipAutoInitialize: true` — for example, in an SSR context where you need to control initialization timing. SecureStore, while\n    // it is used to encrypt/decrypt values stored in AsyncStorage.\n    class LargeSecureStore {\n      private async _encrypt(key: string, value: string) {\n        const required\';\r\n  end if;\r\n  return caller;\r\nend;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nrevoke all on function public.mcp_require_admin() from public, anon;\r\ngrant execute on function public.mcp_require_admin() to await admin.auth.admin.updateUserById(class_id, {\n+          ban_duration: ARCHIVE_BAN_DURATION,\n+        });\n+        if (banError) throw banError;\n+\n+        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: archived[0] });\n+      }\n+\n+      // Restoring an archived account only re-enables sign-in and puts it back\n+      // on the roster. Its feature flags remain off until the teacher grants\n+      // the access deliberately.\n+      if (action === \"unarchive_student\") {\n+        const { class_id } = body;\n+        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: \"Invalid params\" }, 400);\n+\n+        const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\n+        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\n+\n+        const { data: restored, error: restoreError } = await admin\n+          .from(\"profiles\")\n+          .update({ archived_at: null })\n+          .eq(\"class_id\", class_id)\n+          .select(\"class_id, archived_at\");\n+        if (restoreError) throw restoreError;\n+        if (!restored || restored.length === 0) {\n+          return jsonRes({ error: \"No profile matched that class_id\" }, 404);\n+        }\n+\n+        const { error: unbanError } = await admin.auth.admin.updateUserById(class_id, {\n+          ban_duration: \"none\",\n+        });\n+        if (unbanError) throw unbanError;\n+\n+        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: restored[0] });\n+      }\n+\n+      // Permanent deletion is intentionally exact-match gated by the UI and\n+      // repeated here. Auth deletion revokes refresh tokens and cascades data\n+      // with foreign keys; the remaining profile/role rows are removed too in\n+      // case this project predates those foreign keys.\n+      if (action === \"delete_student\") {\n+        const { class_id, confirm_email } = body;\n+        if (!class_id || typeof confirm_email !== \"string\") {\n+          return jsonRes({ error: \"Invalid params\" }, 400);\n+        }\n+\n+        const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\n+        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\n+        if (!target.email || confirm_email.trim().toLowerCase() !== target.email.toLowerCase()) {\n+          return jsonRes({ error: \"Confirmation email did not match\" }, 400);\n+        }\n+\n+        const { error: deleteAuthError } = await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(class_id, false);\n+        if (deleteAuthError) throw deleteAuthError;\n+\n+        const { error: deleteProfileError } = await admin\n+          .from(\"profiles\")\n+          .delete()\n+          .eq(\"class_id\", class_id);\n+        if (deleteProfileError) throw deleteProfileError;\n+\n+        const { error: deleteRoleError } = await admin\n+          .from(\"user_roles\")\n+          .delete()\n+          .eq(\"user_id\", class_id);\n+        if (deleteRoleError) throw deleteRoleError;\n+\n+        return jsonRes({ success: true });\n+      }\n \n       // Set role\n*** Add File: C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\supabase\\migrations\\20260923070000_student_archive.sql\n+-- A reversible account archive needs a durable roster state in addition to\n+-- feature flags. Auth blocking itself lives in admin-manage-users, because\n+-- GoTrue's ban state is not part of the public schema.\n+alter table public.profiles\n+  add column if not exists archived_at timestamptz;\n+\n+create index if not exists profiles_archived_at_idx\n+  on public.profiles (archived_at)\n+  where archived_at is not null;\n*** End Patch";
const result = await tools.apply_patch(patch);

archived[0] });\n+      }\n+\n+      // Restoring an archived account only re-enables sign-in and puts it back\n+      // on the roster. Its feature flags remain off until the teacher grants\n+      // the access deliberately.\n+      if (action === "unarchive_student") {\n+        const { class_id } = body;\n+        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\n+\n+        const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\n+        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\n+\n+        const { data: restored, error: restoreError } = await admin\n+          .from("profiles")\n+          .update({ archived_at: null })\n+          .eq("class_id", class_id)\n+          .select("class_id, archived_at");\n+        if (restoreError) throw restoreError;\n+        if (!restored || restored.length === 0) {\n+          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);\n+        }\n+\n+        const { error: unbanError } = await admin.auth.admin.updateUserById(class_id, {\n+          ban_duration: "none",\n+        });\n+        if (unbanError) throw unbanError;\n+\n+        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: restored[0] });\n+      }\n+\n+      // Permanent deletion is intentionally exact-match gated by the UI and\n+      // repeated here. Auth deletion revokes refresh tokens and cascades data\n+      // with foreign keys; the remaining profile/role rows are removed too in\n+      // case this project predates those foreign keys.\n+      if (action === "delete_student") {\n+        const { class_id, confirm_email } = body;\n+        if (!class_id || typeof confirm_email !== "string") {\n+          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\n+        }\n+\n+        const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\n+        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\n+        if (!target.email || confirm_email.trim().toLowerCase() !== target.email.toLowerCase()) {\n+          return jsonRes({ error: "Confirmation email did not match" }, 400);\n+        }\n+\n+        const { error: deleteAuthError } = await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(class_id, false);\n+        if (deleteAuthError) throw deleteAuthError;\n+\n+        const { error: deleteProfileError } = await admin\n+          .from("profiles")\n+          .delete()\n+          .eq("class_id", class_id);\n+        if (deleteProfileError) throw deleteProfileError;\n+\n+        const { error: deleteRoleError } = await admin\n+          .from("user_roles")\n+          .delete()\n+          .eq("user_id", class_id);\n+        if (deleteRoleError) throw deleteRoleError;\n+\n+        return jsonRes({ success: true });\n+      }\n+\n       // Set role\n       if (action === "set_role") {\n         const { user_id, role } = body;\n'}]

NOT EXISTS profiles (\r\n  class_id text PRIMARY KEY,\r\n  overall_answered int DEFAULT 0,\r\n  overall_correct int DEFAULT 0,\r\n  overall_avg_ms int DEFAULT 0,\r\n  by_qtype_json jsonb DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can view own profile" ON profiles FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can insert own profile" class_id text PRIMARY KEY,\r\n  queue_json jsonb DEFAULT \'[]\'::jsonb,\r\n  cursor_index int DEFAULT 0,\r\n  current_qid text,\r\n  started_at timestamptz DEFAULT now(),\r\n  elapsed_ms int DEFAULT updated_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE sessions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can view own session" ON sessions FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can insert own session" ON sessions FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can update own session" ON sessions FOR UPDATE USING (true);\r\n\r\n-- Settings table\r\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (\r\n  class_id text PRIMARY KEY,\r\n  enabled_qtypes text[] DEFAULT ARRAY[]::text[],\r\n  enabled_levels int[] DEFAULT ARRAY[1,2,3,4,5]::int[],\r\n  adaptive_on boolean DEFAULT true,\r\n  explore_ratio decimal DEFAULT 0.15,\r\n  pace_vs_challenge decimal DEFAULT 0,\r\n  time_pref text DEFAULT \'balanced\',\r\n  updated_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE settings ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can view own settings" ON settings FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can insert own settings" ON settings FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can update own settings" ON settings FOR UPDATE USING (true);\r\n\r\n-- Events table\r\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  class_id text NOT NULL,\r\n  event text NOT NULL,\r\n  details jsonb DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\r\n  timestamp_iso timestamptz DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can view own events" ON events FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can insert own events" ON events FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);\r\n\r\n-- Wrong Answer Journal table\r\nCREATE TABLE public.wrong_answer_journal (\r\n  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  class_id TEXT NOT NULL,\r\n  qid TEXT NOT NULL,\r\n  pt INTEGER NOT NULL,\r\n  section INTEGER NOT NULL,\r\n  qnum INTEGER NOT NULL,\r\n  qtype TEXT NOT NULL,\r\n  level INTEGER NOT NULL,\r\n  first_wrong_at_iso TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  last_status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (last_status IN (\'wrong\', \'right\')),\r\n  revisit_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  history_json JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT \'[]\'::jsonb,\r\n  created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT now(),\r\n  UNIQUE(class_id, qid)\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE public.wrong_answer_journal ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can view own WAJ entries" ON public.wrong_answer_journal FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can insert own WAJ entries" ON public.wrong_answer_journal FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can update own WAJ entries" ON public.wrong_answer_journal FOR UPDATE USING (true);\r\nCREATE TRIGGER update_waj_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON public.wrong_answer_journal FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();\r\n\r\n-- Concept Library table\r\nCREATE TABLE concept_library (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  concept_name text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  reasoning_type text,\r\n  category text,\r\n  explanation text NOT NULL,\r\n  keywords text[],\r\n  application text,\r\n  examples text,\r\n  related_concepts text[],\r\n  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE concept_library ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Knowledge is public" ON concept_library FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\n\r\n-- Question Type Strategies table\r\nCREATE TABLE question_type_strategies (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  question_type text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  category text NOT NULL,\r\n  stem_keywords text[],\r\n  reading_strategy text NOT NULL,\r\n  answer_strategy text NOT NULL,\r\n  correct_answer_patterns text,\r\n  wrong_answer_patterns text,\r\n  prephrase_goal text,\r\n  related_reasoning_types text[],\r\n  difficulty_indicators text,\r\n  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE question_type_strategies ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Strategies are public" ON question_type_strategies FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\n\r\n-- Reasoning Type key_indicators text[],\r\n  common_flaws text[],\r\n  strengthen_tactics text,\r\n  weaken_tactics text,\r\n  relevant_question_types text[],\r\n  examples text,\r\n  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE reasoning_type_guidance ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Guidance is public" ON reasoning_type_guidance FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\n\r\n-- Tactical Patterns table\r\nCREATE TABLE tactical_patterns (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  pattern_name text NOT NULL,\r\n  pattern_type text NOT NULL,\r\n  reasoning_type text,\r\n  question_types text[],\r\n  description text NOT NULL,\r\n  formula text,\r\n  application text,\r\n  examples text,\r\n  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE tactical_patterns ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Patterns are public" ON tactical_patterns FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\n\r\n-- Achievements table\r\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS achievements (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  class_id text NOT NULL,\r\n  badge_id text NOT NULL,\r\n  unlocked_at timestamptz DEFAULT now(),\r\n  progress jsonb DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\r\n  UNIQUE(class_id, badge_id)\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE achievements ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can view own achievements" ON achievements FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can insert own achievements" ON achievements FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can update own achievements" ON achievements FOR UPDATE USING (true);\r\n\r\n-- Flagged Questions table\r\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flagged_questions (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  class_id text NOT NULL,\r\n  qid text NOT NULL,\r\n  pt int NOT NULL,\r\n  section int NOT NULL,\r\n  qnum int NOT NULL,\r\n  qtype text NOT NULL,\r\n  level int NOT NULL,\r\n  note text,\r\n  flagged_at timestamptz DEFAULT now(),\r\n  UNIQUE(class_id, qid)\r\n);\r\nALTER TABLE flagged_questions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can view own flags" ON flagged_questions FOR SELECT USING (true);\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students can insert own flags" ON An archived account remains\r\n// recoverable, but cannot sign in until an admin explicitly restores it.\r\nconst ARCHIVE_BAN_DURATION = "876000h";\r\n\r\nconst ALL_FLAGS = [\r\n  "has_bootcamp_access",\r\n  "has_classroom_access",\r\n  "has_analytics_access",\r\n  "has_schedule_access",\r\n  "has_practice_access",\r\n  "has_drill_access",\r\n  "has_waj_access",\r\n  "has_flagged_access",\r\n  "has_chat_access",\r\n  "has_export_access",\r\n  "has_ta_access",\r\n  "has_journal_access",\r\n  "has_activity_access",\r\n];\r\n\r\nasync function verifyAdmin(req: Request) {\r\n  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!;\r\n  const serviceRoleKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!;\r\n  const anonKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!;\r\n\r\n  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");\r\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) throw new Error("Missing auth");\r\n  const token = authHeader.slice("Bearer ".length);\r\n\r\n  const anonClient = createClient(supabaseUrl, anonKey);\r\n  const { data: claimsData, error } = await anonClient.auth.getClaims(token);\r\n  if (error || !claimsData?.claims?.sub) throw new Error("Unauthorized");\r\n  const user = { id: claimsData.claims.sub as string };\r\n\r\n  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey);\r\n  const { data: roleData, error: roleLookupError } = await admin\r\n    .from("user_roles")\r\n    .select("role")\r\n    .eq("user_id", user.id)\r\n    .eq("role", "admin")\r\n    .maybeSingle();\r\n\r\n  if (roleLookupError) {\r\n    console.error("verifyAdmin: role lookup failed", {\r\n      user_id: user.id,\r\n      error: roleLookupError,\r\n    });\r\n    throw new Error("Forbidden");\r\n  }\r\n  if (!roleData) throw new Error("Forbidden");\r\n\r\n  return { admin, callerId: user.id };\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction jsonRes(data: unknown, status = 200) {\r\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {\r\n    status,\r\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function getManagedUser(admin: any, userId: string) {\r\n  const { data, error } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(userId);\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  if (!data.user) throw new Error("User not found");\r\n  return data.user;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction assertNotPrimaryAdmin(email: string | undefined) {\r\n  if ((email || "").toLowerCase() === PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL) {\r\n    throw new Error("The primary admin account cannot be archived or deleted.");\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {\r\n    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  try {\r\n    const { admin } = await verifyAdmin(req);\r\n\r\n    // ── GET: list users + analytics ──\r\n    if (req.method === "GET") {\r\n      const url = new URL(req.url);\r\n      const mode = url.searchParams.get("mode");\r\n\r\n      // Analytics mode\r\n      if (mode === "analytics") {\r\n        const { data: profiles } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .select("class_id, last_seen_at, " + ALL_FLAGS.join(", "));\r\n\r\n        const { data: { users: authUsers } } = await admin.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });\r\n\r\n        const now = new Date();\r\n        const dayAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);\r\n        const weekAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);\r\n\r\n        const { data: roles } = await admin.from("user_roles").select("user_id, role");\r\n        const adminCount = (roles || []).filter((r: any) => r.role === "admin").length;\r\n\r\n        const activeToday = (profiles || []).filter((p: any) =>\r\n          p.last_seen_at && new Date(p.last_seen_at) > dayAgo\r\n        ).length;\r\n\r\n        const activeWeek = (profiles || []).filter((p: any) =>\r\n          p.last_seen_at && new Date(p.last_seen_at) > weekAgo\r\n        ).length;\r\n\r\n        // Feature adoption counts\r\n        const adoption: Record<string, number> = {};\r\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) {\r\n          adoption[flag] = (profiles || []).filter((p: any) => p[flag] === true).length;\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        // Recent attempts stats\r\n        const { count: totalAttempts } = await admin\r\n          .from("attempts")\r\n          .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true });\r\n\r\n        const { count: weekAttempts } = await admin\r\n          .from("attempts")\r\n          .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })\r\n          .gte("timestamp_iso", weekAgo.toISOString());\r\n\r\n        return jsonRes({\r\n          total_users: authUsers?.length || 0,\r\n          active_today: activeToday,\r\n          active_week: activeWeek,\r\n          admin_count: adminCount,\r\n          feature_adoption: adoption,\r\n          total_attempts: totalAttempts || 0,\r\n          week_attempts: weekAttempts || 0,\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Default: list users\r\n      const { data: profiles, error: pErr } = await admin\r\n        .from("profiles")\r\n        .select("class_id, display_name, last_seen_at, archived_at, " + ALL_FLAGS.join(", "));\r\n      if (pErr) throw pErr;\r\n\r\n      const { data: { users: authUsers }, error: aErr } = await admin.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });\r\n      if (aErr) throw aErr;\r\n\r\n      const emailMap = new Map(authUsers.map((u: any) => [u.id, u.email]));\r\n\r\n      const { data: roles } = await admin.from("user_roles").select("user_id, role");\r\n      const roleMap = new Map<string, string>();\r\n      for (const r of roles || []) {\r\n        roleMap.set(r.user_id, r.role);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const result = (profiles || []).map((p: any) => ({\r\n        ...p,\r\n        email: emailMap.get(p.class_id) || "unknown",\r\n        role: roleMap.get(p.class_id) || "user",\r\n      }));\r\n\r\n      return jsonRes(result);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // ── POST: toggle flag, bulk actions, role management ──\r\n    if (req.method === "POST") {\r\n      const body = await req.json();\r\n      const { action } = body;\r\n\r\n      // Toggle single flag\r\n      if (!action || action === "toggle") {\r\n        const { class_id, field, value } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id || !ALL_FLAGS.includes(field) || typeof value !== "boolean") {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n        // Return the row we actually wrote. A filter that matches nothing is\r\n        // not an error in PostgREST, so without this the dashboard would show\r\n        // a successful toggle that never landed and then snap back on reload.\r\n        const { data: updated, error } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update({ [field]: value })\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id)\r\n          .select("class_id, " + field);\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {\r\n          console.error("toggle: no profile matched", { class_id, field, value });\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);\r\n        }\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: updated[0] });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Apply a template (explicit flag map) to a user. One DB update so\r\n      // the user never sits in a partially-applied state.\r\n      if (action === "apply_template") {\r\n        const { class_id, flags } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id || !flags || typeof flags !== "object") {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n        const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};\r\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) {\r\n          if (typeof flags[flag] === "boolean") {\r\n            updates[flag] = flags[flag];\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n        if (Object.keys(updates).length === 0) {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No valid flags" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n        const { error } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update(updates)\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id);\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Bulk grant/revoke all flags for a user\r\n      if (action === "bulk_user") {\r\n        const { class_id, value } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id || typeof value !== "boolean") {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n        const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};\r\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = value;\r\n        const { error } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update(updates)\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id);\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Put an account back to the state a brand-new signup is in: no intake\r\n      // answers, no name, the intake email re-armed, and every paid door shut.\r\n      // Built for testing the signup path end to end without hand-written SQL,\r\n      // and for the case where a student needs to answer the intake again.\r\n      //\r\n      // Deliberately does not touch auth: the account, its password and its\r\n      // work all survive. Nothing here deletes a student\'s practice history.\r\n      if (action === "reset_onboarding") {\r\n        const { class_id, lock = true } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n\r\n        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {\r\n          onboarding: null,\r\n          onboarding_completed_at: null,\r\n          onboarding_notified_at: null,\r\n          display_name: null,\r\n        };\r\n        if (lock) {\r\n          for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = false;\r\n          // The three a locked account still needs: somewhere to stand, a way\r\n          // to reach Joshua, and a way to book.\r\n          updates.has_classroom_access = true;\r\n          updates.has_chat_access = true;\r\n          updates.has_schedule_access = true;\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        const { data: reset, error } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update(updates)\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id)\r\n          .select("class_id, display_name, onboarding_completed_at, onboarding_notified_at");\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        if (!reset || reset.length === 0) {\r\n          console.error("reset_onboarding: no profile matched", { class_id });\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);\r\n        }\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: reset[0] });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Archive is reversible, but it is a real account lockout: every\r\n      // product flag is closed, the account leaves the active roster, and\r\n      // Supabase Auth rejects future sign-ins until it is restored.\r\n      if (action === "archive_student") {\r\n        const { class_id } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n\r\n        const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\r\n        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\r\n\r\n        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {\r\n          archived_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n        };\r\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = false;\r\n\r\n        const { data: archived, error: archiveError } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update(updates)\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id)\r\n          .select("class_id, archived_at");\r\n        if (archiveError) throw archiveError;\r\n        if (!archived || archived.length === 0) {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        const { error: banError } = await admin.auth.admin.updateUserById(class_id, {\r\n          ban_duration: ARCHIVE_BAN_DURATION,\r\n        });\r\n        if (banError) throw banError;\r\n\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: archived[0] });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Restoring an archived account only re-enables sign-in and puts it back\r\n      // on the roster. Its feature flags remain off until the teacher grants\r\n      // the access deliberately.\r\n      if (action === "unarchive_student") {\r\n        const { class_id } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n\r\n        const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\r\n        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\r\n\r\n        const { data: restored, error: restoreError } = await admin\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .update({ archived_at: null })\r\n          .eq("class_id", class_id)\r\n          .select("class_id, archived_at");\r\n        if (restoreError) throw restoreError;\r\n        if (!restored || restored.length === 0) {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        const { error: unbanError } = await admin.auth.admin.updateUserById(class_id, {\r\n          ban_duration: "none",\r\n        });\r\n        if (unbanError) throw unbanError;\r\n\r\n        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: restored[0] });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Permanent deletion is intentionally exact-match gated by the UI and\r\n      // repeated here. Auth deletion revokes refresh tokens and cascades data\r\n      // with foreign keys; the remaining profile/role rows are removed too in\r\n      // case this project predates those foreign keys.\r\n      if (action === "delete_student") {\r\n        const { class_id, confirm_email } = body;\r\n        if (!class_id || typeof confirm_email !== "string") {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\r\n        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\r\n        if (!target.email || confirm_email.trim().toLowerCase() !== target.email.toLowerCase()) {\r\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Confirmation email did not match" }, 400);\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        const { error: deleteAuthError } = await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(class_id, false);\r\n        if (deleteAuthError) throw deleteAuthError;\r\n\r\n'}]

Ignored when\r\n   * `requireAdmin` is true.\r\n   */\r\n  flag?: PermissionFlag | readonly PermissionFlag[];\r\n  /**\r\n   * Allow any signed-in user through even when the feature flag is false.\r\n   * Useful for core student workspace pages that should stay reachable while\r\n   * their contents are still constrained by row-level data access.\r\n   *\r\n   * "Signed in is enough" — never "nobody needs to be signed in". A route that\r\n   * should be public is not wrapped in this component at all.\r\n   */\r\n  allowSignedIn?: boolean;\r\n  /**\r\n   * When true, only admin users may render the children. Non-admin\r\n   * users, regardless of feature flags, see LockedModule.\r\n   */\r\n  requireAdmin?: boolean;\r\n  children: ReactNode;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst LOCAL_DEV_HOSTS = new Set(["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1"]);\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Opt-in only. A plain `npm run dev` now exercises the real permission path, so\r\n * a gating regression is visible locally instead of surfacing after deploy.\r\n * Set VITE_DEV_BYPASS_GATES=1 in .env.local to walk gated routes without the\r\n * matching flags.\r\n *\r\n * It relaxes *flags*, never authentication — the caller applies it only after\r\n * the signed-in check, so no configuration can make this component render to an\r\n * anonymous visitor.\r\n */\r\nfunction isLocalDevAccessAllowed() {\r\n  return (\r\n    import.meta.env.DEV &&\r\n    import.meta.env.VITE_DEV_BYPASS_GATES === "1" &&\r\n    LOCAL_DEV_HOSTS.has(window.location.hostname)\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport default function ProtectedRoute({ flag, requireAdmin, allowSignedIn, children }: ProtectedRouteProps) {\r\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\r\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\r\n  const location = useLocation();\r\n\r\n  // Auth first, and nothing before it. `useUserPermissions` reports\r\n  // `loading: authReady && !!user && isPending`, which is *false* while auth is\r\n  // still resolving — so a check on `!user` alone read "not yet known" as\r\n  // "signed out" and bounced every cold deep link to /auth. Holding here until\r\n  // auth is definitively resolved is what makes an assignment link survive.\r\n  if (!authReady) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center">\r\n        <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin" />\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Authentication is intrinsic to this component: every shape of it requires a\r\n  // user. The check used to run only for `requireAdmin` or flagged routes, so a\r\n  // wrapper with `allowSignedIn` and no flag fell through to the unconditional\r\n  // render at the bottom and served protected content to anonymous visitors.\r\n  if (!user) {\r\n    const returnTo = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`;\r\n    return <Navigate to={authUrlWithReturnTo(returnTo)} replace />;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // After the auth checks, never before: the dev bypass opens locked features\r\n  // on a local machine, not the front door.\r\n  if (isLocalDevAccessAllowed()) {\r\n    return <>{children}</>;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Only a true cold read blocks; cached reads and background refetches keep\r\n  // this false, so gated navigations do not flash.\r\n  if (permissions.loading) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center">\r\n        <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin" />\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (requireAdmin) {\r\n    return permissions.is_admin ? <>{children}</> : <LockedModule />;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (allowSignedIn) {\r\n    return <>{children}</>;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const flags = Array.isArray(flag) ? flag : flag ? [flag] : [];\r\n  if (flags.length > 0) {\r\n    if (permissions.is_admin || flags.some((f) => permissions[f])) {\r\n      return <>{children}</>;\r\n    }\r\n    return <LockedModule />;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return <>{children}</>;\r\n}\r\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import { useEffect } from "react";\r\nimport { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";\r\nimport { useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";\r\nimport { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";\r\nimport { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";\r\nimport { authUrlWithReturnTo } from "@/lib/authRedirect";\r\n\r\nexport interface UserPermissions {\r\n  has_bootcamp_access: boolean;\r\n  has_classroom_access: boolean;\r\n  has_analytics_access: boolean;\r\n  has_schedule_access: boolean;\r\n  has_practice_access: boolean;\r\n  has_drill_access: boolean;\r\n  has_waj_access: boolean;\r\n  has_journal_access: boolean;\r\n  has_flagged_access: boolean;\r\n  has_chat_access: boolean;\r\n  has_export_access: boolean;\r\n  has_ta_access: boolean;\r\n  has_activity_access: boolean;\r\n  is_admin: boolean;\r\n  loading: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst ACCESS_FLAGS = [\r\n  "has_bootcamp_access",\r\n  "has_classroom_access",\r\n  "has_analytics_access",\r\n  "has_schedule_access",\r\n  "has_practice_access",\r\n  "has_drill_access",\r\n  "has_waj_access",\r\n  "has_journal_access",\r\n  "has_flagged_access",\r\n  "has_chat_access",\r\n  "has_export_access",\r\n  "has_ta_access",\r\n  "has_activity_access",\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type PermissionFlag = (typeof ACCESS_FLAGS)[number];\r\n\r\n// Narrow session-death classifier. Only three conditions count:\r\n//  1. supabase.auth.getSession() returns no session while context says user is set\r\n//  2. Supabase query error with HTTP status 401\r\n//  3. Error message mentioning JWT / token-expired / invalid_token explicitly\r\n// Everything else (including RLS 403s and null profile rows) is legitimate\r\n// permission-denied and must fall through to the normal LockedModule path.\r\nfunction isSessionDeadError(err: any): boolean {\r\n  if (!err) return false;\r\n  if (err.status === 401) return true;\r\n  const msg = String(err.message || "").toLowerCase();\r\n  return (\r\n    msg.includes("jwt") ||\r\n    msg.includes("token is expired") ||\r\n    msg.includes("token expired") ||\r\n    msg.includes("invalid_token")\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\ntype PermissionFlags = Omit<UserPermissions, "loading">;\r\n\r\nconst DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS: PermissionFlags = {\r\n  has_bootcamp_access: false,\r\n  has_classroom_access: false,\r\n  has_analytics_access: false,\r\n  has_schedule_access: false,\r\n  has_practice_access: false,\r\n  has_drill_access: false,\r\n  has_waj_access: false,\r\n  has_journal_access: false,\r\n  has_flagged_access: false,\r\n  has_chat_access: false,\r\n  has_export_access: false,\r\n  has_ta_access: false,\r\n  has_activity_access: false,\r\n  is_admin: false,\r\n};\r\n\r\n// Sentinel thrown by the query fn when the session is dead. The hook reacts to\r\n// it once (sign out + /auth bounce) instead of navigating from inside the\r\n// queryFn, which may run detached from a render.\r\nclass SessionExpiredError extends Error {}\r\n\r\nexport function useUserPermissions(): UserPermissions {\r\n  const { user, authReady, signOut } = useAuth();\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const location = useLocation();\r\n\r\n  const query = useQuery<PermissionFlags>({\r\n    // Cached per user, shared across all ProtectedRoute / consumer mounts, so\r\n    // navigating between gated pages reads cached permissions synchronously\r\n    // instead of re-fetching (and re-showing the full-screen loading gate).\r\n    queryKey: ["user-permissions", user?.id],\r\n    enabled: authReady && !!user,\r\n    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,\r\n    gcTime: 30 * 60_000,\r\n    // Retry transient failures a couple times, but never retry a dead session\r\n    // (that path signs the user out).\r\n    retry: (count, err) => !(err instanceof SessionExpiredError) && count < 2,\r\n    queryFn: async () => {\r\n      const userId = user!.id;\r\n\r\n      // Pre-check: is the session still alive on the client?\r\n      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();\r\n      if (!session) throw new SessionExpiredError();\r\n\r\n      // Fetch access flags from profile\r\n      const { data: profile, error: profileErr } = await supabase\r\n        .from("profiles")\r\n        .select(ACCESS_FLAGS.join(", "))\r\n        .eq("class_id", userId)\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n      if (isSessionDeadError(profileErr)) throw new SessionExpiredError();\r\n      // A non-session read failure (transient network / 5xx) must NOT be\r\n      // silently converted to all-false permissions; throw so TanStack Query\r\n      // records the error and retries instead of caching bogus denied flags.\r\n      if (profileErr) throw profileErr;\r\n\r\n      // Fetch admin role\r\n      const { data: roleData, error: roleErr } = await supabase\r\n        .from("user_roles")\r\n        .select("role")\r\n        .eq("user_id", userId)\r\n        .eq("role", "admin")\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n      if (isSessionDeadError(roleErr)) throw new SessionExpiredError();\r\n      if (roleErr) throw roleErr;\r\n\r\n      const profileRow = (profile ?? null) as unknown as Record<string, boolean> | null;\r\n      const flagValues = { ...DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS };\r\n      for (const flag of ACCESS_FLAGS) {\r\n        flagValues[flag] = profileRow?.[flag] ?? false;\r\n      }\r\n      flagValues.is_admin = !!roleData;\r\n\r\n      // Touch last_seen_at (fire-and-forget). Non-blocking, but check the\r\n      // returned error and warn rather than discarding it entirely.\r\n      supabase\r\n        .from("profiles")\r\n        .update({ last_seen_at: new Date().toISOString() })\r\n        .eq("class_id", userId)\r\n        .then(({ error }) => {\r\n          if (error) console.warn("[permissions] last_seen_at touch failed:", error.message);\r\n        });\r\n\r\n      return flagValues;\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // A dead session bounces to /auth once. Preserve lsatu_saved_email so\r\n  // re-login is smooth after an expiry bounce, and carry where they were: a\r\n  // bare "/auth" discarded the pathname, query, and hash, so a student whose\r\n  // token expired on an assignment link lost the link as well as the session.\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (query.error instanceof SessionExpiredError) {\r\n      const returnTo = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`;\r\n      (async () => {\r\n        await signOut({ keepSavedEmail: true });\r\n        navigate(authUrlWithReturnTo(returnTo), {\r\n          replace: true,\r\n          state: { reason: "session_expired" },\r\n        });\r\n      })();\r\n    } else if (query.error) {\r\n      console.error("Failed to fetch permissions", query.error);\r\n    }\r\n    // `location` is intentionally not a dependency: this must capture where the\r\n    // user was when the session died, not follow them as they navigate.\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\r\n  }, [query.error, signOut, navigate]);\r\n\r\n  return {\r\n    ...DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS,\r\n    ...(query.data ?? {}),\r\n    // Only the true cold load (no cached data) blocks; cached reads and\r\n    // background refetches keep loading false, so gated navigations don\'t flash.\r\n    loading: authReady && !!user && query.isPending,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\n'}]

## assistant

I also found one important nuance: banning an account stops future authentication, but an already-open browser session can remain alive briefly. I’m adding an app-side archived-account check so an active session is also signed out as soon as the profile is read.

const patch = "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\supabase\\functions\\admin-manage-users\\index.ts\n@@\n function assertNotPrimaryAdmin(email: string | undefined) {\n@@\n }\n+\n+async function assertManageableStudent(admin: any, classId: string, email: string | undefined) {\n+  assertNotPrimaryAdmin(email);\n+  const { data, error } = await admin\n+    .from(\"user_roles\")\n+    .select(\"role\")\n+    .eq(\"user_id\", classId)\n+    .eq(\"role\", \"admin\")\n+    .maybeSingle();\n+  if (error) throw error;\n+  if (data) throw new Error(\"Admin accounts cannot be archived, restored, or deleted here.\");\n+}\n@@\n         if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: \"Invalid params\" }, 400);\n \n         const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\n-        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\n+        await assertManageableStudent(admin, class_id, target.email);\n@@\n         if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: \"Invalid params\" }, 400);\n \n         const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\n-        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\n+        await assertManageableStudent(admin, class_id, target.email);\n@@\n \n         const target = await getManagedUser(admin, class_id);\n-        assertNotPrimaryAdmin(target.email);\n+        await assertManageableStudent(admin, class_id, target.email);\n*** Update File: C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\supabase\\migrations\\20260923070000_student_archive.sql\n@@\n create index if not exists profiles_archived_at_idx\n   on public.profiles (archived_at)\n   where archived_at is not null;\n+\n+-- Some older LSAT U tables identify a student solely by class_id instead of\n+-- a foreign key to auth.users. Auth deletion cannot cascade from those rows,\n+-- so remove every public class_id record in a single database transaction.\n+-- This deliberately leaves operational booking and payment records alone:\n+-- they are not platform-work records and do not use class_id.\n+create or replace function public.purge_student_account_data(p_class_id text)\n+returns void\n+language plpgsql\n+security definer\n+set search_path = public\n+as $$\n+declare\n+  target record;\n+begin\n+  for target in\n+    select c.table_schema, c.table_name\n+    from information_schema.columns c\n+    join information_schema.tables t\n+      on t.table_schema = c.table_schema\n+      and t.table_name = c.table_name\n+    where c.table_schema = 'public'\n+      and c.column_name = 'class_id'\n+      and t.table_type = 'BASE TABLE'\n+  loop\n+    execute format('delete from %I.%I where class_id = $1', target.table_schema, target.table_name)\n+      using p_class_id;\n+  end loop;\n+\n+  delete from public.user_roles where user_id::text = p_class_id;\n+end;\n+$$;\n+\n+revoke all on function public.purge_student_account_data(text) from public, anon, authenticated;\n*** End Patch";
const r = await tools.apply_patch(patch);

## My request:
&#x20;Thank you. Okay. What I want to do is make sure that when they archive it, their data doesn't go away. Let me know what needs to be sent to Lovables and make sure it's done in one shot.


## assistant

Archive already preserves student data. It only records the archived state, turns access off, and blocks login. Permanent deletion remains the only action that removes platform work.

I’m preparing this as one Lovable deployment: the database migration plus the account-management function and the session lockout behavior.

It uses SendInput, UI Automation, and Windows.Graphics.Capture screenshots that work even when windows are occluded.\n\nIf this plugin is available, read this entire `SKILL.md` once before Windows automation work, before saying Computer Use is unavailable, and before falling back to other Windows automation.\n\nStart with the directions in the Initialize section below. Read these bundled Markdown files relative to this `SKILL.md` when you need information about the specific topic they cover:\n\n- `../../docs/guidance.md`: core runtime behavior, target-window workflow, screenshot handling, and recovery guidance. You MUST read this before controlling Windows apps.\n- `../../docs/api.md`: full `sky` API reference. Read this when you need method signatures or object shapes.\n- `../../docs/confirmations.md`: you MUST read this before deciding whether a Windows UI action needs confirmation\n\n## Initialize\r\n\r\nThe bundled `cua_node` `@oai/sky` package is the core entry point for Computer Use. Import it directly by package name from the JavaScript session. Do not spawn `codex-computer-use.exe`, search for the helper executable, or build a custom helper protocol client.\r\n\r\nRun this once per fresh `node_repl` JavaScript session:\r\n\r\n```js\r\nif (!globalThis.sky) {\r\n  const { sky } = await import("@oai/sky");\r\n  globalThis.sky = sky;\r\n}\r\n```\n\r\n'}]

Store cross-cell values on `globalThis`; top-level `const` and `let` names cannot be redeclared by later retries.\n- For text output, call `nodeRepl.write(...)` with a string. Use `JSON.stringify(...)` for objects.\n\n## Workflow\n\n### Initialize target selection\n\nRun the Initialize setup cell from `SKILL.md` first. Then list apps and choose the target from returned app and window objects:\n\n```js\nglobalThis.apps = await sky.list_apps();\nnodeRepl.write(JSON.stringify(apps, null, 2));\n```\n\nNever reconstruct an app or window from guessed fields. Do not call `get_window`, `activate_window`, or any input method until selection has produced exactly one returned window.\n\n```js\n{\n  function escapeRegExp(value) {\n    return value.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, "\\\\$&");\n  }\n\n  function returnedWindowSummary(window) {\n    return {\n      id: window.id,\n      app: window.app,\n      title: window.title,\n    };\n  }\n\n  function requireUniqueWindow(windows, label) {\n    if (windows.length !== 1) {\n      nodeRepl.write(\n        `Returned candidate windows:\\n${JSON.stringify(windows.map(returnedWindowSummary), null, 2)}`,\n      );\n      throw new Error(`Expected exactly one target window for ${label}; found ${windows.length}`);\n    }\n    return windows[0];\n  }\n\n  globalThis.apps = await sky.list_apps();\n  globalThis.targetApp = apps.find((app) => app.id === "<app id>");\n  if (!targetApp) throw new Error("Target app was not returned by list_apps");\n  const targetAppId = targetApp.id;\n\n  if (targetApp.windows.length === 0) {\n    await sky.launch_app({ app: targetApp.id });\n    globalThis.apps = await sky.list_apps();\n    globalThis.targetApp = apps.find((app) => app.id === targetAppId);\n  }\n  if (!targetApp?.windows.length) {\n    throw new Error("Target app did not expose a window after launch");\n  }\n\n  const windowTitleHint = "<optional exact window title>";\n  const candidateWindows =\n    windowTitleHint === "<optional exact window title>"\n      ? targetApp.windows\n      : targetApp.windows.filter((window) =>\n          new RegExp(`^${escapeRegExp(windowTitleHint)}$`, "i").test(window.title ?? ""),\n        );\n  const returnedWindow = requireUniqueWindow(candidateWindows, targetApp.id);\n\n  globalThis.targetWindow = await sky.get_window({\n    id: returnedWindow.id,\n    app: returnedWindow.app,\n  });\n  await sky.activate_window({ window: targetWindow });\n  globalThis.state = await sky.get_window_state({ window: targetWindow });\n  globalThis.targetWindow = state.window;\n}\n```\n\nUse `list_windows()` when inspecting currently open windows or recovering a known running app. If the intended app is absent from `list_apps`, launch it with an explicit `.exe` path or `.exe` process identifier, refresh `list_apps()` or `list_windows()`, filter to the intended returned windows, and stop unless the filtered list has exactly one window. Escape Windows path backslashes in JavaScript strings, for example `await sky.launch_app({ app: "C:\\\\Users\\\\me\\\\build\\\\MyApp.exe" });`.\n\n### Act and refresh\n\nUse a two-cell loop for state-derived inputs: observe and stop, inspect the result, then perform exactly one action and refresh immediately. Element indexes, screenshot IDs, and coordinates are valid only for the observation that produced them. Interleaving or retry requires re-observation.\n\nAccessibility path, cell 1: observe and inspect.\n\n```js\nglobalThis.state = await sky.get_window_state({\n  window: targetWindow,\n  include_screenshot: false,\n  include_text: true,\n});\nglobalThis.targetWindow = state.window;\nnodeRepl.write(String(state.accessibility?.tree || state.accessibility?.document_text || ""));\n```\n\nStop here and inspect the emitted tree before choosing an index.\n\nAccessibility path, cell 2: one action and refresh.\n\n```js\n{\n  const observation = globalThis.state;\n  if (observation?.accessibility == null) {\n    throw new Error("No accessibility observation;
